Disoriented\nWithout a compass, we cannot orient our maps!=ZH=迷失方向\n沒有指南針，我們就無法確定地圖的方向！

Magic User=ZH=魔法師
Warrior=ZH=戰士
The base fighter-type in The Bard's Tale, warriors can use nearly every weapon there is. For every 4 levels of experience after the 1st, Warriors get an extra attack ability in combat.=ZH=作為《冰城傳奇》的核心戰鬥職業，戰士可以使用幾乎所有類型的武器。自 1 級之後，戰士每升 4 級即可在戰鬥中獲得一次額外攻擊機會。
Paladin=ZH=聖騎士
Paladins are fighters who have sworn to abstain from all evil and to uphold honor and purity in all places. They can use most weapons and some that no other fighters can. They get multiple attacks at higher levels. They also have a greatly increased resistance to evil magic.=ZH=聖騎士是發誓遠離一切邪惡，並在世間捍衛榮譽與純潔的戰士。他們能使用絕大多數的武器，甚至包含其他戰鬥職業無法企及的神聖兵刃。隨著等級提升，他們將獲得多次攻擊的能力；此外，他們對邪惡魔法也擁有極高的抗性。
Rogue=ZH=盜賊
A professional thief with so-so combat ability, the rogue can hide in shadows, and leap out at distant enemies to land critical hits. They can also search for traps and disarm them. Without a rogue your party will pay very dearly for the booty it wins.=ZH=盜賊是戰鬥能力平平的專業竊賊，但他們能潛藏於陰影之中，並飛躍突襲遠處的敵人以造成致命一擊。他們也能搜尋並解除陷阱。如果隊伍中沒有盜賊，你們為了奪取戰利品而付出的代價，將會無比慘痛。
Bard=ZH=吟遊詩人
The Bard is a wandering minstrel. You'll see them with a tankard of ale in front of them in the less reputable taverns -- the rowdier the better. Their song magic can help your party immensely in and out of combat.=ZH=吟遊詩人是四處漂泊的流浪樂師。你經常能看見他們端著大口麥酒，混跡在那些名聲狼藉的酒館裡——而且地方越喧鬧，他們就越自在。他們的歌聲蘊含著神祕魔力，無論在戰鬥中或冒險旅途上，都能為你的隊伍帶來無比巨大的助益。
Hunter=ZH=獵人
An assassin, a mercenary, a ninja. The hunter can use most weapons, and has the ability (which grows with experience) to do critical hits in combat (i.e., to attack a nerve center or other vital area and instantly kill an opponent). A good skill.=ZH=他是刺客，是僱傭兵，也是忍者。獵人能熟練使用絕大多數的武器，並擁有隨經驗增長而提升的「致命一擊」能力（意即能精準打擊神經中樞或其他要害，瞬間擊殺對手）。這是一門相當了得的本領。
Monk=ZH=武僧
A martial artist, an almost inhuman fighting machine trained to fight without weapons or armor. The monk can use them, but, at higher levels particularly, often does better without.=ZH=他是武術大師，是個受過嚴苛訓練、在不穿盔甲也不帶武器時仍形同非人般恐怖的戰鬥機器。武僧雖然可以使用裝備，但特別是在高等級之後，赤手空拳的他們往往能發揮出更強大的戰力。
Conjurer=ZH=幻術師
Conjurers deal in the physical creation and manifestation of real things (like fire, light, healing).=ZH=幻術師擅長將實體物質具現化。他們能憑空創造並召喚出真實存在之物（例如火焰、光芒與治癒神蹟）。
Magician=ZH=魔術師
Magicians deal with magic as it affects physical objects (i.e., enchanting a sword, making armor stronger, making a dungeon wall disappear).=ZH=魔術師精通改變物質型態與附加附魔的法術。他們能對實體目標施加魔力影響（例如：為長劍附魔、強化盔甲的防禦力，或是令地下城的牆壁憑空消失）。
Sorcerer=ZH=術士
Sorcerers are magic users who deal with the creation and manipulation of illusion. Due to the power of sorcerer spells, this class is not available to newly created characters.=ZH=術士是專精於創造與操控幻象的施法者。由於術士法術蘊含的威力過於強大，此職業並不開放給初出茅廬的新創角色。
Wizard=ZH=巫師
Wizards are magic users who are dedicated to the summoning and binding of various supernatural creatures. These creatures are not friendly to humankind and trying to control them is extremely hazardous. The Wizard-class is not available to new characters either.=ZH=巫師是潛心於召喚與束縛各類超自然生物的施法者。這些異界生物對人類毫無友善之情，試圖掌控牠們是極其凶險的舉動。與術士相同，巫師職業同樣不開放給初出茅廬的新角色。
Archmage=ZH=大魔導師
These magic users have learned at least three levels for each of the previous four magic user classes. The Archmage is one of the most powerful and important characters in The Destiny Knight because an Archmage can do almost anything. Remember, Lagoth Zanta is an Archmage... fight fire with fire.=ZH=大魔導師是指在先前四種施法者職業中，每一門都至少研習至第 3 階（級）以上的魔法大師。大魔導師是《命運騎士》中最強大且最關鍵的角色之一，因為他們幾乎無所不能。請牢記，大魔王拉哥斯·贊塔也是一名大魔導師……想要擊敗他，你就得以毒攻毒，以烈火對抗烈火。
Chronomancer=ZH=時空術士
Chronomancers are the travel experts of the magic users. Before becoming one, a magic user must master all the spells of any three mage classes. And once you become a Chronomancer, you lose the power to use all the spells you previously learned. It may sound unfair, but the Chronomancer needs to focus all of his or her attention on the important dimension teleport spells. Don't think you can get around without this spellcaster, because you just can't.=ZH=時空術士是所有施法者當中的「旅者專家」。在晉升此職業前，施法者必須先精通任意三種法師職業的所有咒語。然而，一旦你成為時空術士，你將會失去先前習得的所有法術。這聽起來或許有些不公，但時空術士必須將所有的專注力，投注在至關重要的維度傳送法術上。千萬別以為沒有這位施法者你也能闖蕩世界——因為你根本寸步難行。
Geomancer=ZH=大地術士
Fighters who want to convert to a life of a magic can become Geomancers. Once they choose to change, they can still use the armor, weapons, and magic items that the fighter classes use. Beware: Bards lose their songs, Warriors lose their multiple attacks, Hunters lose their critical hit ability, and Monks lose their armor class bonus and multiple attacks. It's part of the trade off. To become a Geomancer, you must find the one special location that performs the rite of passage to this mage class.=ZH=渴望皈依魔法之道的戰鬥職業，可以轉職為「大地術士」。一旦選擇轉變，他們仍可繼續裝備戰士階級所使用的盔甲、武器與魔法道具。然而切記：轉職後，吟遊詩人將失去吟唱樂曲的能力、戰士會失去多次攻擊的技巧、獵人將失去致命一擊的本領，而武僧則會失去防禦防具加成與多次攻擊的優勢。這便是力量交換的代價。想要成為大地術士，你必須在世間尋獲那處舉行此法系晉升儀式的專屬祕境。
Monster=ZH=怪物
Illusion=ZH=幻象
Human=ZH=人類
While possibly being of hardier stock, this character is nonetheless like you or me.=ZH=「儘管他們的體魄或許更為強健，但這類角色本質上就如同你我一般，只是個普通人。」
Elf=ZH=精靈
Patterned after the Tolkien elf, the elf is slight of build, frequently taller and nimbler than a human, and very inclined to magic.=ZH=「精靈是以托爾金筆下的形象為藍本塑造。他們身形纖細，通常比人類更為高挑與敏捷，且天生對魔法有著極高的親和力。」
Dwarf=ZH=矮人
The Dwarven people are short and stout, extremely strong and healthy, but not amazingly intelligent (i.e. excellent fighters).=ZH=「矮人一族身材矮小而粗壯，他們擁有極其強悍的怪力與健康體魄，但在智力方面的表現就沒那麼驚人了（換句話說，他們是無可挑剔的頂尖戰士）。」
Hobbit=ZH=哈比人
Hobbits are slightly smaller than Dwarves but are nimble and dexterous. Just the right make-up for a rogue.=ZH=「哈比人的體型比矮人還要稍顯嬌小，但他們身手極其矯健且心思靈巧。這般天賦異稟的體質，簡直是為了成為盜賊而量身打造。」
Half-Elf=ZH=半精靈
These crossbreeds are usually blond and fair-skinned, like elves, and get some added size and strength from their human ancestry.=ZH=「這些混血兒通常擁有如精靈般耀眼的金色髮絲與白皙膚色，同時也從人類的血統中，繼承了更為高大的身形與強健的力量。」
Half-Orc=ZH=半獸人
An orc is a large, goblin-like creature often found working for evil wizards. The Half-orc, being half human, is not quite as despicable as his orc parent, but you wouldn't want to date one. They are tough but incredibly stupid.=ZH=「獸人是一種體型龐大、外貌酷似哥布林的生物，經常被發現受僱於邪惡的巫師。而半獸人因為擁有一半的人類血統，雖然不像他們的獸人父母那般面目可憎，但你絕對不會想跟他們約會。他們皮糙肉厚，卻也蠢得無可救藥。」
Gnome=ZH=侏儒
Gnomes closely resemble dwarves, but have less hair and even shorter tempers. They are also more magically inclined, as a rule. Highly intelligent but not very robust.=ZH=「地精的外貌與矮人極其相似，但他們的毛髮較少，脾氣也更為暴躁。一般而言，他們對魔法有著更高的天賦。雖然智力超群，但他們的體魄卻相當脆弱。」
Male=ZH=男性
Female=ZH=女性
Strength=ZH=力量
Strength is physical power and chiefly affects the amount of damage a character can do to an opponent in hand-to-hand combat. Make sure your fighting characters are strong.=ZH=力量代表角色的肉體勁道。它主要影響角色在近身肉搏戰中，能對敵人造成多少傷害。請務必確保你隊伍中的戰鬥職業擁有強健的力量。
Intelligence=ZH=智力
Intelligence is mental power. A high intelligence will enable your magic users to get bonus spell points.=ZH=智力代表角色的精神心智力量。卓越的智力能讓你的施法者獲得額外的法力值加成。
Dexterity=ZH=敏捷
Measures agility & nimbleness. A high score makes your characters harder to hit and helps them strike the first blow in combat.=ZH=本項數值用以評估角色的身手與靈敏度。高昂的敏捷度不僅能讓角色更難被敵人擊中，還能助他們在戰鬥中搶佔先機、率先出招。
Constitution=ZH=體質
Measures healthiness. It takes more damage to kill a character with a high constitution score. This is reflected in bonus "hit points", the character's life span.=ZH=本項數值用以評估角色的健康與強韌度。體質優異的角色需要承受更多傷害才會倒下，這將直接體現於額外的「生命值」加成上，亦即延展了角色的生命極限。
Luck=ZH=幸運
Luck is an ambiguous attribute, as it has a number of unseen effects on gameplay. For example, lucky characters are more likely to resist evil magic and avoid nasty traps.=ZH=幸運是一項難以捉摸的屬性，因為它對遊戲過程有著諸多潛移默化的隱形影響。舉例來說，受到命運眷顧的角色，將更有機會抵抗邪惡魔法的侵襲，並完美避開那些陰險毒辣的機關陷阱。
Hit Points=ZH=生命值
Hit Points measure the damage a character can take before they are killed. The current status is shown in the characters status bar, followed by their current maximum potential.=ZH=生命值用以衡量角色在力竭戰死前所能承受的傷害總量。當前的生命狀態會顯示在角色的狀態欄中，其後則會標註他們目前所能達到的最大生命潛能值。
Spell Points=ZH=法術點數
This shows current spell point status. Points are used up with each cast of a magic spell. If a Conjurer has 20 spell points and casts a Mage Flame spell at a cost of 2 spell points, his new total would be 18. The characters status line lists both their current and maximum spell points.=ZH=本項數值顯示當前的法力值狀態。每當施展魔法咒語時，便會消耗相應的點數。舉例來說：若一名幻術師擁有 20 點法力值，並施展了消耗 2 點法力的「法師之火」咒語，其剩餘的法力總量將會變為 18 點。角色的狀態欄會同時列出他們當前的法力值與最大法力上限。

<color=#ffff80>All</color>\nShow all the spells that this caster currently knows. Filter them further by typing keywords below.=ZH=<color=#ffff80>全部</color>\n顯示該施法者目前已知的所有法術。透過在下面輸入關鍵字進一步過濾它們。
<color=#ffff80>Exploration</color>\nSpells that help the party during their travels, such as creating light, detecting the unseen, aid in movement or disarm traps.=ZH=<color=#ffff80>探索</color>\n在旅途中幫助隊伍的法術，例如創造光、探測看不見的東西、幫助移動或解除陷阱。
<color=#ffff80>Healing</color>\nSpells that replenish hit points, cure status conditions, remove curses or resurrect the party members.=ZH=<color=#ffff80>治療</color>\n補充生命值、治療狀態狀況、消除詛咒或復活隊員的法術。
<color=#ffff80>Buffs</color>\nSpells that increase the strength, armor or general battle skills of individuals or the entire the party.=ZH=<color=#ffff80>增益</color>\n增加個人或整個隊伍的力量、護甲或一般戰鬥技能的法術。
<color=#ffff80>Single Target</color>\nSpells that do direct damage to a single enemy.=ZH=<color=#ffff80>單一目標</color>\n對單一敵人造成直接傷害的法術。
<color=#ffff80>Multiple Targets</color>\nSpells that do damage to one or more groups of enemies.=ZH=<color=#ffff80>多個目標</color>\n對一組或多組敵人造成傷害的法術。
<color=#ffff80>Curses</color>\nSpells that reduce the combat effectiveness of enemies, or inflict negative status conditions on the enemies such as poison.=ZH=<color=#ffff80>詛咒</color>\n降低敵人戰鬥力或對敵人造成負面狀態（例如中毒）的法術。
<color=#ffff80>Summoning</color>\nSpells that summon monsters or allies to join your party.=ZH=<color=#ffff80>召喚</color>\n召喚怪物或盟友加入你的隊伍的法術。

Divine Intervention=ZH=神聖幹預
This powerful spell earns its name by doing the following: 1) Turns illusionary characters into real characters; 2) Cures characters of all illnesses but age; and 3) Restores all hit points to the party. If you're in combat, it also does the following: 1) Lowers your armor class, saving throw, to hit, and damage by 20 points; 2) Increases your attack by eight points; and 3) Casts Mangar's Mallet.=ZH=這個強大的咒語因執行以下操作而得名：1）將幻象角色變成真實角色； 2）治療除老年以外的一切疾病； 3) 恢復隊伍的所有生命值。如果你在戰鬥中，它還會執行以下操作：1）降低你的護甲等級、豁免檢定、命中率和傷害 20 點； 2）攻擊力增加8點； 3) 鑄造曼加爾的木槌。
Dragon Breath=ZH=龍息
Allows the mage to breathe fire at a group of foes, doing {0}-{1} points of damage to each.=ZH=允許法師向一群敵人噴火，對每個敵人造成{0}-{1}點傷害。
DreamSpell=ZH=夢咒
This powerful spell earns its name by doing the following: 1) Turns illusionary characters into real characters; 2) Cures characters of all illnesses but age; and 3) Restores all hit points to the party. If you're in combat, it also does the following: 1) Lowers your armor class, saving throw, to hit, and damage by 20 points; 2) Increases your attack by eight points; and 3) Casts Mangar's Mallet. When used out of combat, the Dreamspell can be used to escape the current dungeon.=ZH=這個強大的咒語因執行以下操作而得名：1）將幻象角色變成真實角色； 2）治療除老年以外的一切疾病； 3) 恢復隊伍的所有生命值。如果你在戰鬥中，它還會執行以下操作：1）降低你的護甲等級、豁免檢定、命中率和傷害 20 點； 2）攻擊力增加8點； 3) 鑄造曼加爾的木槌。當在戰鬥之外使用時，夢咒可以用來逃離當前的地下城。

Target-Dummy=ZH=目標虛擬
A magical illusion appears in the party. Unable to attack, it will serve to draw enemy attacks to himself.=ZH=聚會中出現了神奇的幻象。無法攻擊，就會吸引敵人的攻擊到自己身上。
The Brothers Kringle=ZH=克林格爾兄弟
The brothers are always ready to help friends in trouble. Enough brothers appear to fill the empty slots in your party.=ZH=兄弟倆總是樂於幫助有困難的朋友。足夠多的兄弟出現來填補你隊伍中的空缺。

Guild Master=ZH=公會會長
As you enter the guild, you catch the eye of the Guild Master.\n\n=ZH=當你進入公會時，你引起了會長的注意。 \n\n
"Well met, brave adventurer, but you come in the most dire of times."\n\n"The evil wizard, Mangar, has cursed our town with this blizzard, and the streets are overrun with all matter of evil miscreants."=ZH=“很高興見到你，勇敢的冒險家，但你在最可怕的時刻到來了。”\n\n“邪惡的巫師曼加爾用這場暴風雪詛咒了我們的城鎮，街道上到處都是邪惡的惡棍。”
"You must help us defeat Mangar! In this guild you will find many skilled warriors and mages that would join you in this quest."\n\n=ZH=「你必須幫助我們擊敗曼加爾！在這個公會中你會發現許多熟練的戰士和法師會加入你的任務。」\n\n
"When your party is ready, set out into town. Be sure to visit Garth's Shoppe to the north and equip yourself for battle. He has the finest weapons and armour in Skara Brae, and indeed is the only shoppe brave enough to open these days!"=ZH=「當你的隊伍準備好後，出發進城。一定要參觀北邊的加斯裝備店，並為戰鬥做好準備。他擁有斯卡拉布雷最好的武器和盔甲，而且確實是這些天唯一有勇氣開業的商店！”
"The city has an abundance of temples that are open night and day to heal your wounds, and I believe Roscoe is still doing good business restoring magic points for your mages."\n\n=ZH=「這座城市有大量的寺廟，日夜開放，可以治癒你的傷口，我相信Roscoe仍然在為你的法師恢復魔法點方面做得很好。」\n\n
"And when your bard gets thirsty, be sure to visit one of the many Inns and Taverns. The Scarlet Bard, to the south, has the best selection in town!"=ZH=「當你的吟遊詩人口渴時，一定要前往眾多旅館和酒館之一。位於南方的猩紅吟遊詩人有鎮上最好的選擇！”
"Now I will bid you good day and good luck in your quest!"\n\nThe guild master smiles and returns to what he was doing.=ZH=「現在我要向你道個美好的一天，祝你的任務好運！」\n\n公會會長微笑著，繼續他正在做的事情。
You have stepped out into the town of Skara Brae for the first time. Would you like a quick tutorial on the game controls before you proceed?=ZH=您第一次踏入斯卡拉布雷鎮。在繼續之前，您想了解有關遊戲控制的快速教學嗎？
Use W,A,S,D to move around the town, or the cursor keys. You can also left click on the game view to move around.\n\nHold down right mouse on the game view and you can look around to take in the view.=ZH=使用 W、A、S、D 在城鎮中移動，或使用遊標鍵。您也可以左鍵點擊遊戲視圖來移動。 \n\n在遊戲視圖上按住滑鼠右鍵，您可以環顧四周以查看視圖。
There is a Pause button at the top left of the game view that gives access to graphics, audio and game settings, as well as saving or loading the game.\n\nThere is an Actions button on the top right of the game view that will drop down a ribbon of actions to choose from. These actions are not usable during game events.=ZH=遊戲視圖的左上角有一個「暫停」按鈕，可以存取圖形、音訊和遊戲設置，以及儲存或載入遊戲。 \n\n遊戲視圖的右上角有一個「動作」按鈕，可以從下拉式選單中選擇操作。這些動作在遊戲活動期間不可用。
You can change the speed that combat messages scroll by pressing the 'greater than' and 'less than' keys, or by adjusting the slider in the 'game settings' menu.=ZH=您可以按下「<」和「>」鍵或調整「遊戲設定」選單中的滑桿來變更戰鬥訊息滾動的速度。
Pressing 1 to 7 lets you view the stats of one of your party members.\n\nIf you left click and drag with your mouse on one of the party member names below, you can change their position in the party. Only the first 4 character slots can be hit by enemy melee attacks.\n\n=ZH=按 1 到 7 可查看一名隊伍成員的統計資料。 \n\n如果您在下面的隊伍成員姓名上左鍵點擊並拖曳滑鼠，您可以變更他們在隊伍中的位置。敵人的近戰攻擊只能擊中前 4 個角色位置。 \n\n
You should try to keep a character slot free so that you can summon creatures or allow roving monsters to join your party.=ZH=你應該試著在隊伍中留出一個空位，以便在冒險中召喚魔法生物，或是接納沿途流浪前來投靠的怪物加入你的陣營。
Below the game view, totals of your party's gold and inventory items are shown.\n\nAny item that is not equipped by a character will go into the communal inventory and is accessible through any player's inventory screen.=ZH=遊戲視圖下方會顯示您隊伍的黃金和庫存物品總數。 \n\n角色未裝備的任何物品都將進入公共庫存，並且可以透過任何玩家的庫存畫面存取。

Saradon's Workshop=ZH=薩拉東的工作室
A long corridor stretches out before you, through walls stacked to the ceiling with books. You can almost feel the magic in the air.=ZH=一條長長的走廊在你面前延伸，牆上堆滿了書籍，一直延伸到天花板。你幾乎可以感受到空氣中的魔力。
A sign on the wall reads:\n\n"Saradon's Workshop. The uninvited are not welcome."=ZH=牆上的標誌上寫著：\n\n“薩拉頓工作室。不歡迎不請自來的人。”
You find a scrap of parchment in the handwriting of Saradon. It reads:\n\nIf you're reading this, you have already proven yourself in my maze of teleports. Respect my workshop please, but take what you need. You may find something of use. Farewell and good luck.=ZH=你發現一小片羊皮紙上有薩拉頓的筆跡。上面寫著：\n\n如果您正在閱讀本文，那麼您已經在我的傳送迷宮中證明了自己。請尊重我的工作室，但拿走你需要的東西。你可能會發現一些有用的東西。再見，祝你好運。
You stumble over an open book on the floor.\n\nIt seems that Saradon maintained a correspondence with Longinus, the mad prophet of Skara Brae. Longinus’ replies seem to have consisted of nothing more than inscrutable rhymes. You pick up one such example and read it...\n\nBy tor and loch by burn and tarn.\nYour fate lies in the lap of Narn.=ZH=你絆倒在地板上一本打開的書。 \n\n薩拉頓似乎與斯卡拉布雷的瘋狂先知朗吉努斯保持著通信。朗吉努斯的回答似乎只不過是一些難以理解的押韻。你拿起一個這樣的例子並閱讀它......\n\n通過托爾和湖通過燃燒和塔恩。 \n你的命運掌握在納恩的懷抱裡。
More notes from Longinus catch your eye, these just as cryptic as the previous\n\nThe greatest heroes number seven.\nAssume their roles and rise to heaven.=ZH=朗吉努斯的更多筆記引起了您的注意，這些筆記與之前的筆記一樣神秘\n\n最偉大的七位英雄。 \n承擔他們的角色併升入天堂。
On a low table there is yet another piece of nonsensical poetry from the mad prophet:\n\nThough high you fly or low you stoop.\nYou will never be worthy of the soup.=ZH=矮桌上還放著另一首來自瘋狂先知的無意義詩：\n\n雖高則飛，低則彎。 \n你永遠配不上這道湯。
You find what seems to be a longer draft of the letter you received from Saradon.=ZH=你發現從薩拉登那裡收到的信的草稿似乎更長。
My name is Saradon, a wizard of some fame and little fortune. Though you know me not, and we have never met, I feel that I have known you all my life. For the story of your deeds, your entanglement with Mangar and his evil horde in Skara Brae, flows from the lips of every Bard throughout the Realm.=ZH=我的名字叫薩拉頓，是一位頗有名氣但財富微薄的巫師。雖然你不認識我，我們也從未見過面，但我覺得我一生都認識你。因為你的事蹟、你與曼加爾以及他在斯卡拉布雷的邪惡部落的糾葛，從整個王國的每個吟遊詩人的嘴裡流傳出來。
For this reason, for your courage and experience, I have contacted you above all others. For now we face an evil much greater than Mangar's. An evil that threatens not the safety and tranquility of but one city, nae... an evil that threatens the existence of the entire Realm and all the cities and citizens within!=ZH=基於這個原因，為了你的勇氣和經驗，我先聯絡了你。現在我們面臨著比曼加爾更大的邪惡。一種邪惡不僅僅威脅一個城市的安全與安寧，不……一種威脅整個王國以及其中所有城市和公民的存在的邪惡！
Recall now the lessons of your childhood. The story of Turin, the Archmage who forged the Destiny Wand in the molten depths of the holy mountain Krontor. The Destiny Wand, the uniting force which has rallied armies to the aid of the Realm, and has maintained peace and prosperity for the last 700 years.=ZH=現在回想一下你童年的教訓。都靈的故事，大法師在克朗托聖山的熔岩深處鑄造了命運魔杖。命運魔杖，集結軍隊援助王國的團結力量，並在過去 700 年裡維持了和平與繁榮。
But look around you now, where has the power of the Destiny Wand gone? Lawless mercenaries from the neighboring kingdom of Lestradae have invaded the Realm and now run rampant through the streets. Even in Tangramayne, our capital! It is they who have taken the Destiny Wand, with the guidance of an evil Archmage known as Lagoth Zanta.=ZH=但現在看看你的周圍，命運魔杖的力量到哪裡去了？來自鄰國萊力量拉德的無法無天的傭兵入侵了王國，現在在街上猖獗。即使在我們的首都唐格拉瑪尼！正是他們在邪惡大法師拉格斯·贊塔的指導下拿走了命運魔杖。
Although Lagoth has thwarted each of my attempts to gain knowledge of his powers and location, I have been able to discover that the Destiny Wand lies broken in seven pieces, at seven different locations. Lagoth has placed each fragment of the scepter within a Snare of Death - a puzzle room that will require all the wisdom and cunning at your disposal in order for you to survive.=ZH=儘管拉戈斯挫敗了我了解他的力量和位置的每一次嘗試，但我已經能夠發現命運魔杖破碎成七塊，分佈在七個不同的位置。拉戈斯將權杖的每個碎片都放置在死亡陷阱中——這是一個謎題室，需要你運用所有的智慧和狡猾才能生存。
Do you believe in legends?\n\nLegend states that, Unimaginable power is bestowed upon the one who reforges the Destiny Wand... Regardless of whether or not you hold faith in the stuff of legend, you must defeat Lagoth Zanta, regain the seven fragments, and reforge the Destiny Wand - it is the only way to save the Realm.=ZH=你相信傳說嗎？ \n\n傳說，重鑄命運魔杖的人會被賦予難以想像的力量……無論你是否相信傳說，你都必須擊敗拉格斯·贊塔，奪回七片碎片，重鑄命運魔杖——這是拯救王國的唯一方法。
Now I must hurry, for in my attempts to glean knowledge of Lagoth, he has discovered my prying magic and even now his mercenaries approach. I care not for my own safety, but I pray that my magic is still strong enough to deliver this letter into your hands.=ZH=現在我必須抓緊時間，因為當我試圖收集拉格斯的知識時，他已經發現了我的窺探魔法，甚至現在他的僱傭兵也已經逼近了。我不在乎自己的安全，但我祈禱我的魔法仍然強大到足以將這封信送到你的手中。
My workshop contains powerful quest knowledge which I have gathered using magic and other, more physical, methods. Use it to help you during your quest. My only regret is that I cannot personally be there to counsel you during your quest. May fate smile upon you... and upon me.\n\nSaradon.=ZH=我的工作室包含強大的任務知識，這些知識是我使用魔法和其他更物理的方法收集的。在你的探索過程中用它來幫助你。我唯一的遺憾是我不能親自在你的探索過程中為你提供建議。願命運向你微笑……也向我微笑。 \n\nSaradon。
You find a staff leaning against the bookshelf.\n\nIt has a note attached to it, which reads:\n\n"This staff harmonizes the magical energies around it and channels them into the wielder, replenishing their spell points.\n\nTake it, for it will surely provide some assistance to you on your journey, adventurers."=ZH=你發現書架上斜靠著一根法杖。 \n\n它上面附有一張紙條，上面寫著：\n\n“這根法杖可以協調周圍的魔法能量，並將它們引導到持有者身上，補充他們的法術點。\n\n拿上它吧，因為它一定會給你的冒險之旅提供一些幫助。”

Some more notes lay here:\n\n"To learn the spell one must take five clues into the stone. But the order, it's all wrong..."=ZH=這裡還有一些註釋：\n\n“要學習該咒語，必須將五個線索放入石頭中。但是順序，全錯了......”
And another page reads:\n\n"The crypt holds the first, and a maze will lead you to the second..."=ZH=另一頁寫著：\n\n“地窖裡有第一個，迷宮會帶你到第二個…”
And another page reads:\n\n"The third lies in a castle, and a fort for the fourth."=ZH=另一頁寫著：\n\n“第三個位於城堡中，第四個位於堡壘中。”
A final page reads:\n\n"A tall tower keeps the final secret."=ZH=最後一頁寫著：\n\n“一座高塔保守著最後的秘密。”
You find a note from Saradon's personal journal.\n\nThe mysterious appearance of the tower to the south is of great concern to me. Combined with the treachery of Oscon's betrayal to Count Waldec, things are at a boiling point. The time has come to seek help from abroad.=ZH=你從薩拉頓的個人日記中找到了一條筆記。 \n\n南邊塔樓的神秘外觀引起了我的極大關注。再加上奧斯康對瓦爾德克伯爵的背叛，事情已經到了沸點。現在是向國外尋求幫助的時候了。

Use W,A,S,D to move around the wilderness, or the cursor keys. You can also left click on the game view to move around.\n\nHold down right mouse on the game view and you can look around to take in the view.=ZH=使用 W、A、S、D 在荒野中移動，或使用遊標鍵。您也可以左鍵點擊遊戲視圖來移動。 \n\n在遊戲視圖上按住滑鼠右鍵，您可以環顧四周以查看視圖。

Outpost=ZH=前哨站
The small hut holds the utter cold at bay, but the wind still whistles through small cracks, and the floor is covered with sparkling snowflakes.\n\n=ZH=小茅屋擋住了嚴寒，但風還是從縫隙裡呼嘯而過，地上鋪滿了晶瑩的雪花。 \n\n
Seated at a desk you see the frozen body of a man, perhaps a half-elf. The weather has not been kind to him, and he is, in effect, mummified so you cannot tell how long he has been here.=ZH=坐在桌子旁，你看到一具冰凍的男人屍體，也許是半精靈。天氣對他來說不太好，而且他實際上已經木乃伊化了，所以你無法判斷他在這裡待了多久。
Despite that confusion about his body's condition, however, the gold wristlets and circlet he wears are styled after the fashion thought over 1000 years old in your world.\n\n=ZH=儘管人們對他的身體狀況感到困惑，但他佩戴的金腕帶和金環是按照你們世界 1000 多年的時尚思想設計的。 \n\n
On the desk you see, beneath a light dusting of snow, a book. Its pages are covered with a bold, flowing hand that, unfortunately, turned into weakened and trembling writing.=ZH=在桌子上，你看到一本書，上面覆蓋著一層薄薄的雪。它的書頁是用大膽、流暢的手寫的，不幸的是，它變成了虛弱和顫抖的文字。
Even a brief glance at it leaves you with the impression this was the dead man's diary.\n\n=ZH=即使只是簡單地看一眼，你也會覺得這是死者的日記。 \n\n

Entry 1 ~\n=ZH=條目 1 ~\n
"The battle goes poorly, but that is as I would have predicted it. This time we have no hero like Hawkslayer to bolster our militia.=ZH=「戰鬥進行得很糟糕，但這正如我所預料的那樣。這次我們沒有鷹殺手這樣的英雄來支持我們的民兵。
The first invasion, as the songs sing, would have succeeded but for Lanatir's vast magics.\n\n=ZH=正如歌曲所唱的那樣，如果沒有拉納提爾強大的魔法，第一次入侵就會成功。 \n\n
How cruel it is, then, that the invaders would have struck Lanatir down in their first attack. Without hero and god, how can we prevail?"=ZH=那麼，入侵者在第一次進攻中就將拉納提爾擊倒，這是多麼殘酷啊。沒有英雄和上帝，我們如何能夠勝利？ 」
Entry 2 ~\n=ZH=條目 2 ~\n
"I agree with my brothers that none of the monsters will penetrate Lanatir's tomb, so his Sphere and the Wand of Power are safe until claimed by those capable of piercing our wards.=ZH=「我同意我的兄弟們的觀點，沒有任何怪物會穿透拉納提爾的墳墓，所以他的球體和力量魔杖是安全的，直到那些能夠穿透我們的守衛的人奪走為止。
It was difficult in creating those wards, powerful magic created them and only the proper spells can destroy them.\n\n=ZH=創造這些結界很困難，強大的魔法創造了它們，只有正確的咒語才能摧毀它們。 \n\n

The enthroned figure pulls out a sword and begins to speak...=ZH=登基者拔出一把劍，開始說話…

This attack will unlock when a creature uses it against your party.=ZH=當一個生物使用它來對抗你的隊伍時，這種攻擊就會解鎖。
Melee attacks deal damage to a single character in the first 4 rows of your party, if their AC is not low enough to resist the attack.=ZH=近戰攻擊會對你隊伍前 4 排中的單一角色造成傷害，前提是他們的 AC 不足以抵抗攻擊。
Ranged attacks can attack any party member from any distance.=ZH=遠程攻擊可以從任何距離攻擊任何隊伍成員。
Breath attacks do damage to all your party members that fail to magically resist.=ZH=吐息攻擊會對所有未能進行魔法抵抗的隊員造成傷害。

Volume I=ZH=首部曲
THE LEGEND OF SKARA BRAE\n\n\n\nLong ago in the land of Caith, when magic still prevailed, the evil wizard Mangar the Dark threatened a small but harmonious town called Skara Brae. Overnight, all the town's militiamen disappeared, and Mangar froze the surrounding countryside with a spell of Eternal Winter, totally isolating it from any possible help. \n\n\n\nAs evil creatures oozed into the sewers to join Mangar’s shadow crusade, the future of Skara Brae hung in the balance. And who was left to resist? Only a handful of unproven young Warriors, some junior magic users, a couple of Bards barely old enough to drink, and a few out of work Rogues.\n\n\n\nYou are the leader of this ragtag group of freedom fighters. Luckily you have a Bard with you to sing your glories - if you survive - for this is the stuff of legends. \n\n\n\nAnd thus the story begins...=ZH=斯卡拉布雷的傳說\n\n\n\n很久以前，在凱斯大陸，魔法盛行的年代，邪惡的黑暗巫師曼加爾威脅著一座名為斯卡拉布雷的小鎮，那裡原本寧靜祥和。一夜之間，鎮上的所有民兵都消失了，曼加爾施展了永冬魔法，將周圍的鄉村冰封，使其與外界徹底隔絕。\n\n\n\n邪惡的生物湧入下水道，加入曼加爾的暗影遠征，斯卡拉布雷的未來岌岌可危。而誰還能挺身而出抵抗呢？只有幾個未經考驗的年輕戰士，一些初級魔法師，幾個剛到法定飲酒年齡的吟遊詩人，以及幾個失業的盜賊。\n\n\n\n你就是這群烏合之眾的領袖。幸運的是，你身邊還有一位吟遊詩人，他會為你歌頌榮耀——如果你能活下來的話——因為這將會成為一段傳奇。\n\n\n\n故事由此展開…
"The song I sing\nwill tell the tale\nof a cold and wintery day"=ZH=“我唱的歌\n將講述一個寒冷冬日的故事”
"When evil fled\nand brave men bled\nthe Dark One came to stay."=ZH=“當邪惡逃走，勇敢的人們流血時，黑暗勢力就會留下來。”
"Of castle walls\nand torchlit halls\nand a price men had to pay."=ZH=“城堡的城牆\n火把照亮的大廳\n以及人們必須付出的代價。”
"Till men of old\nfor blood and gold\nhad rescued Skara Brae."=ZH=“直到古人\n為了血和黃金\n拯救了斯卡拉·布雷。”
Volume II=ZH=二部曲
A LETTER RECEIVED\n\n\n\nGreetings, heroes! My name is Saradon, a wizard of some fame and little fortune. Every bard in Caith sings of your victory over Mangar and his vile horde in Skara Brae. Thus, even though we have never met, I am seeking you out.\n\n\n\nOur land now faces a danger even greater than Mangar - an evil that could leave it open to invasion and bloody conquest. The Destiny Wand, which has kept us safe for seven hundred years, has been shattered into seven fragments by the Archmage Lagoth Zanta, and those fragments hidden all across the realm. Until you recover them, reforge the wand, and defeat Lagoth Zanta, Caith is in peril. Save her, I beg you.\n\n\n\nNow I must hurry, for while I was attempting to glean knowledge of Zanta, he sensed my prying magic, and even now his Lestradae mercenaries approach. I care not for my own safety, but I pray my magic will keep this letter safe until it reaches your hands. May fate smile upon you... and me.=ZH=收到一封信 \n \n \n \n 英雄們，你好！我的名字叫薩拉頓，是一位頗有名氣但財富微薄的巫師。凱斯的每一位吟遊詩人都在歌頌你對斯卡拉布雷的曼加爾和他的邪惡部落的勝利。因此，尽管我们从未见过面，但我正在寻找你。 \n \n \n \n 我們的土地現在面臨著比曼加爾更大的危險 - 一種可能使其容易遭受入侵和血腥征服的邪惡。七百年来保障我们安全的命运魔杖已被大法师拉戈斯·赞塔打碎成七块碎片，这些碎片隐藏在整个王国的各个角落。在你找回它們、重鑄魔杖並擊敗拉戈斯·贊塔之前，凱斯將處於危險之中。救救她，我求你了。 \n \n \n \n 现在我必须快点，因为当我试图收集赞塔的知识时，他感觉到了我的窥探魔法，甚至现在他的雷力量拉德雇佣兵也接近了。我不關心自己的安全，但我祈禱我的魔法能確保這封信的安全，直到它到達你的手中。願命運對你和我微笑。
Volume III=ZH=三部曲
As I lay dying...\n\n\n\nI leave this record so those who come after will know what horrors that the Mad God Tarjan has perpetrated, and so that they will not be lulled into believing that Skara Brae was his sole target.\n\n\n\nWe held a great festival to celebrate the victory over Mangar. Then Mangar's Master, The Mad God Tarjan, arrived... His shadow ravaged the city and foul creatures burst forth to assault fair Skara Brae. People ran, but Tarjan's minions left none untouched.\n\n Tarjan gloated that Skara Brae was merely one in a series of conquests that would complete his revenge. He laughed when I promised that the legendary Hawkslayer, and those who defeated Mangar, would return again to triumph. As payment for my threat, he gave a swift twist of his jewelled sword into my belly. \n\n\n\nTarjan must be stopped, for upon this rests the fate of more than Skara Brae, more than Caith. Failure will mean the extinction of all life - save for the wretched Tarjan and his servile parasites. Pray for success... and life renewed.=ZH=当我弥留之际…… \n \n \n \n 我留下这张记录，是为了让后来的人知道疯神塔尔詹犯下了多么恐怖的罪行，这样他们就不会误以为斯卡拉·布雷是他唯一的目标。 \n \n \n \n 我们举行了盛大的节日来庆祝对曼加尔的胜利。然后曼加尔的主人，疯狂之神塔尔詹到来了……他的影子蹂躏了这座城市，邪恶的生物突然袭击了美丽的斯卡拉布雷。人們四散奔逃，但塔里安的爪牙無一倖免。 \n \n Tarjan 幸灾乐祸地认为 Skara Brae 只是完成他复仇的一系列征服中的一个。當我承諾傳奇的鷹手和那些擊敗曼加爾的人將再次凱旋時，他笑了。作為對我威脅的回應，他將寶石劍迅速地扭入我的腹部。  \n \n \n \n 塔里安必須被阻止，因為這不僅僅關係到斯卡拉·布雷和凱斯的命運。失敗將意味著所有生命的滅絕——除了可憐的塔爾詹和他的奴隸寄生蟲。祈禱成功……並讓生活煥然一新。

His praise washes over you like a warm ocean wave, and\n\nyou feel strength infuse your body. "In doing what you have\n\ndone, you have proven yourself worthy of nothing less than\n\nthe ultimate reward." Quitting now will lose any unsaved progress because you cannot autosave outside of the Guild while Legacy Mode Save-At-Guild is active in Tales of the Unknown or The Destiny Knight. Do you wish to quit anyway?=ZH=現在退出將失去所有未保存的進度，因為當傳統模式公會保存在《未知故事》或《命運騎士》中處於活動狀態時，您無法在公會之外自動保存。你還是想退出嗎？
Quitting now will lose any unsaved progress because you cannot autosave during an active death snare event. Do you wish to quit anyway?=ZH=現在退出將丟失所有未保存的進度，因為您無法在活躍的死亡陷阱事件期間自動保存。你還是想退出嗎？
Quitting now will lose any unsaved progress because you cannot autosave during combat. Do you wish to quit anyway?=ZH=現在退出將丟失所有未儲存的進度，因為您無法在戰鬥中自動儲存。你還是想退出嗎？

You cannot save during special events, or shops=ZH=您無法在特殊活動或商店期間節省費用
Each character has their own inventory and gold to manage. Gold and items will need to be pooled and traded between characters. Each character can hold up to 16 items at a time, less if Misc Game Differences is enabled.=ZH=每個角色都有自己的庫存和黃金需要管理。黃金和物品需要在角色之間匯集和交易。每個角色一次最多可以持有 16 個物品，如果啟用“雜項遊戲差異”，則數量會更少。
With Save At Guild Only enabled, saving the game will only be possible when the party are in an adventurers guild or camp. Autosave will only fire when entering a guild. With Misc Game Differences also enabled, BT3 will still allow saving anywhere, since the original game introduced a save game option.=ZH=啟用「僅在公會保存」後，只有當隊伍位於冒險家公會或營地時才能保存遊戲。自動儲存只會在進入公會時觸發。在啟用其他遊戲差異的情況下，BT3 仍然允許在任何地方保存，因為原始遊戲引入了保存遊戲選項。
Enables differences between each game. These include:\n\n- SPECIAL SLOT for monsters only in BT1.\n\n- No archery in BT1.\n\n- No item charges in BT1, instead items have a small chance to disappear on each use.\n\n- 8 slot inventory in BT1 and 10 slots in BT2.\n\n- Wine Skins only available in BT3.\n\n- Rogue identifying items only available in BT3.\n\n- Items and Spells adjusted to match each game.=ZH=啟用每個遊戲之間的差異。其中包括： \n \n - 僅適用於 BT1 中怪物的特殊欄位。 \n \n - BT1 中沒有射箭。 \n \n - BT1 中沒有物品費用，相反，物品在每次使用時都有很小的機會消失。 \n \n - BT1 中有 8 個欄位庫存，BT2 中有 10 個欄位。 \n \n - 酒皮僅在 BT3 中提供。 \n \n - 盜賊識別物品僅在 BT3 中可用。 \n \n - 調整物品和法術以匹配每個遊戲。
Enable the full XP requirements from the originals, and also some exploits that helped with levelling.\n\n- Up to twice the XP needed for each level.\n\n- Allows party attack to be initiated anywhere.\n\n- Enabled shop buy and sell any charged item.\n\n- All game events and battles are now redoable.=ZH=啟用原版的完整 XP 要求，以及一些有助於升級的漏洞。 \n \n - 每個等級所需經驗值最多兩倍。 \n \n - 允許在任何地方發動團體攻擊。 \n \n - 允許商店購買和出售任何收費物品。 \n \n - 所有遊戲事件和戰鬥現在都可以重做。
Grab some grid paper and map the levels yourself. This was a big part of the 1980s experience and why all the spinners and teleports in dungeons exist. For those that have the time and temperament, this can be a rewarding experience. If Misc Game Differences is also enabled, then the <b>Thief of Fate (BT3)</b> will still allow for a simplified journal, just showing map walls and doors, as the original game introduced an automap feature.=ZH=拿一些網格紙並自己繪製水平圖。這是 20 世紀 80 年代體驗的重要組成部分，也是地下城中所有旋轉器和傳送裝置存在的原因。對於那些有時間和性情的人來說，這可能是一次有益的經驗。如果也啟用了其他遊戲差異，那麼<b>Thief of Fate (BT3)</b>仍將允許簡化日誌，僅顯示地圖牆和門，因為原始遊戲引入了自動地圖功能。
Enabling this setting restores the way song magic used to work in the originals.\n\nSongs in <b>Tales of the Unknown (BT1)</b> and <b>The Destiny Knight (BT2)</b> revert to having their strength vary with dungeon difficulty. Songs in the <b>Thief of Fate (BT3)</b> have their strength vary with level of the bard that is singing.=ZH=啟用此設定將恢復歌曲魔法在原作中的工作方式。 \n\n<b>未知故事 (BT1)</b>和<b>命運騎士 (BT2)</b>中的歌曲恢復為根據地下城難度而改變其強度。<b>Thief of Fate (BT3)</b>中的歌曲的強度會根據演唱者的等級而變化。
On some platforms, such as the Commodore 64 (C64), it was possible to use items, that were not equipped, in combat. This has an especially large impact on bards, choosing to blow horns, or magic users in the <b>Thief of Fate (BT3)</b> using harmonic gems. Enabling this option also removes the ability to equip or unequip items during combat.=ZH=在某些平台上，例如 Commodore 64 (C64)，可以在戰鬥中使用未裝備的物品。這對選擇吹號角的吟遊詩人或使用和諧寶石的<b>命運之賊 (BT3)</b>中的魔法使用者有特別大的影響。啟用此選項還會消除在戰鬥中裝備或取消裝備物品的能力。
The remaster adds some hints and random gear rewards to be found by searching the empty houses in cities. Ticking this option will remove those and empty houses will just be for finding random groups of enemies to fight, or the occasional dungeon entrance. This makes gaining the first few character levels just a little harder.=ZH=重製版添加了一些提示和隨機裝備獎勵，可以透過搜尋城市中的空房子找到。勾選此選項將刪除這些空房子，而空房子將僅用於尋找隨機的敵人群體進行戰鬥，或偶爾的地下城入口。這使得獲得前幾個角色等級變得有點困難。

Your first three dungeons should be the sewers, the catacombs and the castle (in that order). Each is progressively tougher.=ZH=你的前三個地下城應該是下水道、地下墓穴和城堡（按順序）。每一個都逐漸變得更加艱難。
You can adjust the speed that messages scroll in combat in gameplay settings.=ZH=您可以在遊戲設定中調整戰鬥中訊息滾動的速度。
Avoid traps. Higher level rogues are excellent at opening chests, but when in doubt use the 'Trapzap' spell.=ZH=避免陷阱。高級盜賊非常擅長打開寶箱，但如果有疑問，請使用“Trapzap”法術。
Be sure that your party is fully healed before entering a new dungeon.=ZH=在進入新的地下城之前，請確保您的隊伍已完全康復。
Remember that each succeeding level goes UP in a tower or castle and DOWN in a dungeon.=ZH=請記住，每個後續關卡在塔樓或城堡中都會上升，而在地下城中則會下降。
Good maps will show you the logical spots for secret doors and secret rooms.=ZH=好的地圖會告訴你秘密門和秘密房間的邏輯位置。
Use your spells and Bard songs to lower the armor class of your entire party. The lower the better.=ZH=使用你的咒語和吟遊詩人歌曲來降低整個隊伍的護甲等級。越低越好。
You can minimize damage by killing off all but one monster in either of the first two attacking groups.=ZH=您可以透過殺死前兩個攻擊組中除一隻以外的所有怪物來最大程度地減少傷害。
Treat the undead with respect. In other words, kill them quickly.=ZH=尊重不死生物。換句話說，就是盡快殺掉他們。
Be prepared to die a lot with level 1 and 2 characters. Especially at night. Especially when you don't have any weapons and are just walking to Garth's Shoppe.=ZH=準備好與 1 級和 2 級角色一起死去很多次。尤其是在晚上。尤其是當你沒有任何武器並且只是步行前往加斯裝備店時。
Monsters are smart. They usually attack your most vulnerable characters first, unless one of your members represents a significant threat.=ZH=怪物很聰明。他們通常會先攻擊你最脆弱的角色，除非你的成員之一構成了重大威脅。
Beware the Doppelganger monster. It enters your party and looks just like one of your members.=ZH=小心雙重怪物。它進入你的隊伍並且看起來像你的一個成員。
Develop a Sorcerer fast. They are very useful in dungeons.=ZH=快速培養魔法師。它們在地下城中非常有用。
Don't venture too far into any dungeon without your maximum spell points. A good rule is to leave a dungeon when you are down to one quarter of your maximum spell points.=ZH=在沒有達到最大法術點的情況下，不要冒險進入任何地下城太遠。一個好的規則是當你的最大法術點減少到四分之一時離開地下城。
Manage your spell points carefully. Don't waste a spell if you can accomplish the same thing without magic.=ZH=仔細管理你的法術點。如果你不用魔法也能完成同樣的事情，就不要浪費法術。
Locate traps, second sight and sorcerer sight spells all identify traps within 30 feet.=ZH=定位陷阱、第二視力和魔法師視力法術都可以識別 30 英尺內的陷阱。
Cast a long lasting Bard spell right before entering a tavern. It's like getting a free spell.=ZH=在進入酒館之前施展永久吟遊詩人咒語。這就像獲得免費咒語一樣。
Generally, the more expensive the item is, the better it works. Almost like real life.=ZH=一般來說，物品越貴，效果越好。幾乎就像現實生活一樣。
There are no cursed or bad items, unlike real life.=ZH=與現實生活不同，這裡沒有被詛咒或壞的物品。
Don't be stingy. Equip your fighters with the best armor, weapon, shield, helmet and gauntlets money can buy. After all, they can't take it with them.=ZH=別小氣。為你的戰士配備金錢能買到的最好的盔甲、武器、盾牌、頭盔和手套。畢竟，他們不能帶走。
Experiment with all items you find to determine their capabilities. Magical items are key to success.=ZH=試驗你找到的所有物品以確定它們的功能。魔法物品是成功的關鍵。
The tavern on Rakhir Street is the only tavern that serves wine. And that wine "goes down" easily. Good luck!=ZH=Rakhir Street 上的小酒館是唯一提供葡萄酒的小酒館。而且這種酒很容易「變質」。祝你好運！
If your spell points aren't recharging fast enough, go see Roscoe. But be prepared for electrifyingly high fees.=ZH=如果你的法術點數恢復得不夠快，就去見Roscoe。但要做好高得驚人的費用的準備。
Temples are divine places of resurrection and complete healing. In fact, this is the only place to cure characters who have been withered or drained of experience.=ZH=聖殿是復活和完全治癒的神聖之地。事實上，這是治癒那些枯萎或經驗耗盡的角色的唯一地方。
Gold you receive in battles is pooled for the party and can be used to buy weapons, heal your wounds or regenerate your spell points.=ZH=你在戰鬥中收到的金幣會匯集到隊伍中，可以用來購買武器、治癒你的傷口或恢復你的法術點。
After battles you may find items which go into a pool for anyone to use. Sell or drop items if your inventory gets too full.=ZH=戰鬥結束後，您可能會發現物品進入池中供任何人使用。如果您的庫存太滿，請出售或丟棄物品。
Every mage should know where Roscoe's Energy Emporium is. He will regenerate your magic if the price is right.=ZH=每個法師都應該知道Roscoe的能量商場在哪裡。如果價格合適，他會恢復你的魔力。
If you cannot kill off all the magic-using monsters, you may want to cast anti-magic spells to protect against illusions, possessions and other spells.=ZH=如果你無法殺死所有使用魔法的怪物，你可能需要施展反魔法法術來防禦幻象、附體和其他法術。
It might be a good idea to stay close to a Temple at night, so you can quickly heal any wounds.=ZH=晚上待在寺廟附近可能是個好主意，這樣你就可以快速治癒傷口。
Wizards are extremely important in advanced levels; they can summon demons, extremely powerful specials.=ZH=巫師在高級關卡中極為重要；他們可以召喚惡魔，極其強大的特殊能力。
Items that appear in the inventory in red cannot be used by that character class.=ZH=該角色類別無法使用庫存中顯示為紅色的物品。
Don't be shy about using magic in combat. When in doubt, blast 'em with magic.=ZH=不要羞於在戰鬥中使用魔法。當有疑問時，用魔法轟炸他們。
Trapzap spell disarms all traps within 30 feet.=ZH=Trapzap 法術可以解除 30 英尺內的所有陷阱。
You may wish to drop by a tavern to wet the whistle of your Bard.=ZH=您可能想順便去一家小酒館喝點吟遊詩人的口哨。
Deeper dungeons require a higher Hide-In-Shadows and Critical Strike skill=ZH=更深的地下城需要更高的隱藏陰影和暴擊技能
There's dungeons deep underneath Skara Brae... sewers, catacombs, the castle and more, each more dangerous than the one before.=ZH=斯卡拉布雷地下深處有地下城……下水道、地下墓穴、城堡等等，每一個都比以前更危險。
Explore and map every square in every maze. There are 'Magic Mouths' that give hints. There are 1-of-a-kind magic items and spell regeneration zones. Good maps will show you the logical spots for secret doors and secret rooms, too.=ZH=探索並繪製每個迷宮中的每個方塊。有給予提示的「魔嘴」。有獨一無二的魔法物品和法術再生區。好的地圖也會向您顯示秘密門和秘密房間的邏輯位置。
Avoid traps. Higher level rogues are excellent at opening chests, but when in doubt use the 'Trapzap' spell.=ZH=避免陷阱。高級盜賊非常擅長打開寶箱，但如果有疑問，請使用“Trapzap”法術。
You can use 'Trapzap' to disarm any traps you encounter.=ZH=您可以使用“Trapzap”來解除您遇到的任何陷阱。
Be sure that your party is fully healed before entering a new dungeon.=ZH=在進入新的地下城之前，請確保您的隊伍已完全康復。
When you are finding your bearings in a maze, remember that each succeeding level goes UP in a tower or castle and DOWN in a dungeon.=ZH=當您在迷宮中找到方向時，請記住，每個後續關卡在塔樓或城堡中都會上升，而在地下城中則會下降。
Your highest dexterity character will attack earliest in combat. Target particularly nasty monsters with spells and attacks from high dexterity characters first. Your less dexterous characters may not survive long enough to get in a first strike.=ZH=你的敏捷度最高的角色將在戰鬥中最早發動攻擊。首先使用高敏捷角色的法術和攻擊來瞄準特別令人討厭的怪物。你的不太靈巧的角色可能無法生存足夠長的時間來發動第一次攻擊。
Use your spells and Bard songs to lower the armor class of your entire party. The lower the better.=ZH=使用你的咒語和吟遊詩人歌曲來降低整個隊伍的護甲等級。越低越好。
If you are attacked by more than 2 groups of monsters, concentrate on magic users first. If you cannot kill off all the magic-using monsters, you may want to cast anti-magic spells to protect against illusions, possessions and other spells.=ZH=如果你受到超過2組怪物的攻擊，首先集中攻擊魔法使用者。如果你無法殺死所有使用魔法的怪物，你可能需要施展反魔法法術來防禦幻象、附體和其他法術。
You can minimize damage by killing off monsters in melee range first. Non-magic users, further than 10' away, will need to advance before they can attack.=ZH=您可以透過先殺死近戰範圍內的怪物來最大程度地減少傷害。距離超過 10 英尺的非魔法用戶需要前進才能攻擊。
Many of the undead monsters are capable of draining experience levels, turning characters old and gray or even turning characters to stone. Treat the undead with respect. In other words, kill them quickly.=ZH=許多不死怪物能夠耗盡經驗值，使角色變老變灰，甚至將角色變成石頭。尊重不死生物。換句話說，就是盡快殺掉他們。
Be prepared to die a lot with level 1 and 2 characters. Especially at night. Especially when you don't have any weapons and are just walking to Garth's Shoppe. It might even be a good idea to stay close to a Temple at night, so you can quickly heal any wounds.=ZH=準備好與 1 級和 2 級角色一起死去很多次。尤其是在晚上。尤其是當你沒有任何武器並且只是步行前往加斯裝備店時。晚上待在寺廟附近甚至可能是個好主意，這樣你就可以快速治癒傷口。
Monsters are smart. They usually attack your most vulnerable characters first, unless one of your members represents a significant threat.=ZH=怪物很聰明。他們通常會先攻擊你最脆弱的角色，除非你的成員之一構成了重大威脅。
Beware the Doppelganger monster. It enters your party and looks just like one of your members.=ZH=小心雙重怪物。它進入你的隊伍並且看起來像你的一個成員。
Develop a Sorcerer fast. They are very useful in dungeons. Wizards are extremely important in advanced levels; they can summon demons, extremely powerful specials. Develop an Archmage, too. You'll need one; you'll want more.=ZH=快速培養魔法師。它們在地下城中非常有用。巫師在高級關卡中極為重要；他們可以召喚惡魔，極其強大的特殊能力。也培養一名大法師。你需要一個；你會想要更多。
Don't venture too far into any dungeon without your maximum spell points. A good rule is to leave a dungeon when you are down to one quarter of your maximum spell points.=ZH=在沒有達到最大法術點的情況下，不要冒險進入任何地下城太遠。一個好的規則是當你的最大法術點減少到四分之一時離開地下城。
Manage your spell points carefully. Don't waste a spell if you can accomplish the same thing without magic. Torches, for example, often work as well as magic light spells. On the other hand, don't be shy about using magic in combat. When in doubt, blast 'em with magic.=ZH=仔細管理你的法術點。如果你不用魔法也能完成同樣的事情，就不要浪費法術。例如，火把通常和魔法光咒一樣有效。另一方面，不要羞於在戰鬥中使用魔法。當有疑問時，用魔法轟炸他們。
Locate traps, second sight and sorcerer sight spells all identify traps within 30 feet. Trapzap spell disarms all traps within 30 feet.=ZH=定位陷阱、第二視力和魔法師視力法術都可以識別 30 英尺內的陷阱。 Trapzap 法術可以解除 30 英尺內的所有陷阱。
Cast a long lasting Bard spell right before entering a tavern. It's like getting a free spell.=ZH=在進入酒館之前施展永久吟遊詩人咒語。這就像獲得免費咒語一樣。
Generally, the more expensive the item is, the better it works.=ZH=一般來說，物品越貴，效果越好。
There are no cursed or bad items.=ZH=沒有被詛咒或壞的物品。
Don't be stingy. Equip your fighters with the best armor, weapon, shield, helmet and gauntlets money can buy. After all, they can't take it with them.=ZH=別小氣。為你的戰士配備金錢能買到的最好的盔甲、武器、盾牌、頭盔和手套。畢竟，他們不能帶走。
Experiment with all items you find to determine their capabilities. Magical items are key to success. Remember that an item may only be usable for certain characters and classes, so trade items around if you have to.=ZH=試驗你找到的所有物品以確定它們的功能。魔法物品是成功的關鍵。請記住，某個物品可能僅適用於某些角色和類別，因此如果有必要，請交換物品。
Seek out the Review Board. You need to visit there to be awarded new levels and spells.=ZH=尋求評審委員會。您需要訪問那裡才能獲得新的等級和法術。
By the way, the tavern on Rakhir Street is the only tavern that serves wine. And that wine "goes down" easily. You may wish to drop by to wet the whistle of your Bard. Good luck!=ZH=順便說一句，拉基爾街上的小酒館是唯一一家提供葡萄酒的小酒館。而且這種酒很容易「變質」。您可能會希望順便來潤濕您的吟遊詩人的口哨。祝你好運！
If your spell points aren't recharging fast enough, go see Roscoe. But be prepared for electrifyingly high fees.=ZH=如果你的法術點數恢復得不夠快，就去見Roscoe。但要做好高得驚人的費用的準備。
Temples are divine places of resurrection and complete healing. In fact, this is the only place to cure characters who have been withered or turned to stone.=ZH=聖殿是復活和完全治癒的神聖場所。事實上，這是治愈枯萎或變成石頭的角色的唯一地方。
Gold you receive in battles is pooled for the party and can be used to buy weapons, heal your wounds or regenerate your spell points.=ZH=你在戰鬥中收到的金幣會匯集到隊伍中，可以用來購買武器、治癒你的傷口或恢復你的法術點。
After battles you may find items which go into a pool for anyone to use. Sell or drop items if your party inventory gets too full.=ZH=戰鬥結束後，您可能會發現物品進入池中供任何人使用。如果您的隊伍背包太滿，請出售或丟棄物品。
Every mage should know where Roscoe's Energy Emporium is. He will regenerate your magic if the price is right.=ZH=每個法師都應該知道Roscoe的能量商場在哪裡。如果價格合適，他會恢復你的魔力。
The temples can heal your wounds, cure your illnesses and even bring back the dead for the right donation.=ZH=寺廟可以治癒你的傷口，治癒你的疾病，甚至可以透過正確的捐贈讓死者復活。
Deeper dungeons require a higher hide-in-shadows skill. Keep upgrading your skill to 100 and beyond or you will be discovered more often.=ZH=更深的地下城需要更高的潛伏陰影技能。不斷將你的技能升級到 100 或更高，否則你會更頻繁地被發現。
Search all the buildings in all the towns. There is a dungeon to be found in every town, starting with Tangramayne.=ZH=搜索所有城鎮的所有建築物。從坦格拉瑪恩開始，每個城鎮都有地下城。
The forests of the Realm hide a variety of interesting buildings, huts and strongholds (as well as a host of evildoers). Crypts can also be found in the wilderness and are worth investigating if you can gain access.=ZH=王國的森林裡隱藏著各種有趣的建築、小屋和堡壘（以及許多邪惡之徒）。荒野中也可以找到地穴，如果您能進入，就值得調查。
Be sure to tip the bartender. He has valuable information to give you. Try giving him more money when you can afford it.=ZH=一定要給調酒師小費。他有寶貴的資訊可以提供給你。當你有能力的時候，試著給他更多的錢。
Searching the room, you discover some parchment from the Adventurer's Guild Handbook. It reads:\n\n=ZH=搜查房間後，你發現了冒險家公會手冊中的一些羊皮紙。內容如下：\n\n

Mangar protects himself using a riddle with 7 answers, and you found them all! Your skills are without limit. You will surely be able to rid us of this evil soon!=ZH=Mangar 用一個有 7 個答案的謎語來保護自己，而你卻全部找到了！你的技能是無限的。你一定能很快除掉我們這個邪惡的！
You found the Mad God and defeated him?! That's amazing. Our faith in you was not wasted!=ZH=你找到了狂神並擊敗了他？ ！太棒了。我們對你的信心沒有白費！
I see you've found an entrance into the catacombs. Your progress is impressive!=ZH=我看到你找到了地下墓穴的入口。你的進步令人印象深刻！
What is this? The statue turned into a Samurai and attacked you? This must be Mangar's evil magic. There are guardian statues throughout the town and some are undoubtedly even tougher to defeat!=ZH=這是什麼？雕像變成武士攻擊你？這一定是曼加爾的邪惡魔法。鎮上到處都有守護者雕像，有些雕像無疑更難擊敗！
An evil wizard Mangar is threatening the town. He's brought down a blizzard upon us and all manner of evil creatures are wandering about.\n\nTake a party out into town, explore everywhere for clues. You MUST find a way to stop him!=ZH=邪惡巫師曼加爾正在威脅小鎮。祂為我們帶來了一場暴風雪，各種各樣的邪惡生物四處遊蕩。 \n\n組隊進城，到處探索尋找線索。你必須想辦法阻止他！
Your party is full. Try removing some members before you add a new member.=ZH=你的隊伍已滿。在新增成員之前嘗試刪除一些成員。

There is nobody in your party, and so you cannot remove a member. Have a good day!=ZH=您的隊伍中沒有人，因此您無法刪除成員。祝你有美好的一天！

Seek out Brilhasti ap Tarj beneath the Mad God's Temple!=ZH=尋找狂神神廟下方的布里哈斯蒂阿普塔吉！
The old man in the Review Board scratches his head. "Yes, you are the prophesied ones, but you have come too early. No matter."=ZH=評審委員會的老者搔著頭。 “是的，你們就是預言者，不過你們來得太早了，無所謂了。”
"Beneath Skara Brae you will find one of Tarjan's devotees. Brilhasti ap Tarj is a foul Necromancer, and his life impedes my efforts to stave off disaster."=ZH=“在斯卡拉布雷的下面，你會發現塔吉安的一位信徒。塔吉的布里哈斯蒂是一位邪惡的死靈法師，他的生命阻礙了我避免災難的努力。”
"You may enter the Catacombs under the Mad God's Temple by uttering his master's name... 'Tarjan'"=ZH=“只要說出他主人的名字……‘塔爾詹’，就可以進入瘋神殿下的地下墓穴。”
"Destroy Brilhasti ap Tarj, then return to me for your true quest."=ZH=“摧毀塔吉地區的布里哈斯蒂，然後回到我這裡來完成你真正的任務。”
"Go to Arboria and retrieve for me Valarian's Bow and The Arrows of Life. The Chronomancer spell, Apport Arboria, cast at the Twilight Copse will get you there. The spell, Reditus Arboria, cast at the point where you arrived, will return you to Caith."=ZH=「去阿爾伯利亞，為我取回瓦拉里安之弓和生命之箭。在暮光小樹林施展時空術士咒語“阿伯利亞”，將帶你到達那裡。在你到達的地方施放咒語“雷迪圖斯·阿爾伯利亞”，將使你回到凱斯身邊。”
"Welcome ye children of the prophecy. Upon your shoulders falls a great weight, for you must embark on what will be your greatest adventure ever."=ZH=“歡迎你們這些預言之子。你們的肩上擔負著沉重的擔子，因為你們必須踏上你們有史以來最偉大的冒險。”
"That which has laid waste to Skara Brae is an ancient evil recently released. It threatens to destroy all reality and time as it has wrought havoc on Skara Brae."=ZH=“摧毀斯卡拉布雷的是一種最近被釋放的古老邪惡。它威脅要摧毀所有現實和時間，因為它對斯卡拉布雷造成了嚴重破壞。”
"If you cannot stop it, it will consume the universe. If you do stop it, you will be rewarded beyond all your dreams."=ZH=“如果你無法阻止它，它將吞噬整個宇宙。如果你阻止它，你將獲得超乎你夢想的回報。”
"Prepare thyselves, and hasten to the place of trees, for it is most like the first dimension you must sojourn in to blunt the evil."=ZH=“做好準備，趕緊前往樹木所在的地方，因為這很像是你必須逗留以削弱邪惡的第一個維度。”
"Arboria, the home of Valarian the bold, is reached through using powerful magic that only a Chronomancer can control."=ZH=“阿爾伯利亞是勇敢者瓦拉倫的故鄉，通過使用只有時空法師才能控制的強大魔法才能到達。”
"Bring to me Valarian's Bow and The Arrows of Life if Valarian will not return here with you."=ZH=「如果瓦拉里安不和你一起回到這裡，就把瓦拉里安的弓和生命之箭帶給我。”
"Yes, and be on the lookout for an ally, for you are not the first I have sent on this quest. Though your paths are different, they may cross, and you will do well together."=ZH=「是的，並且要尋找盟友，因為你不是我第一個派去執行此任務的人。雖然你們的道路不同，但它們可能會交叉，而且你們在一起會做得很好。”
"Use Apport Gelidia at Cold Peak to travel to Gelidia and retrieve for me the Wand of Power and the Sphere of Lanatir."=ZH=“使用冷峰的阿普特·吉利迪亞前往吉利迪亞並為我取迴力量魔杖和拉納提爾球體。”
The old man takes the news of Valarian's death hard, but he summons a smile to his lips and breathes deeply.=ZH=老人對瓦拉里安過世的消息感到很難過，但他嘴角揚起微笑，深深噴吐了一口氣。
"You have done well, children, and I take well the news that Hawkslayer has survived this long. Now you are bound for a place far distant."=ZH=“你們做得很好，孩子們，鷹殺手活了這麼久的消息我很高興。現在你們要前往一個遙遠的地方。”
"It is the dimension of magic. It is known as Gelidia and if your Chronomancer is able, I will share the spells to get you there and back again."=ZH=「這是魔法的維度。它被稱為 Gelidia，如果你的時空法師有能力，我將分享咒語，讓你再次到達那裡並返回。”
"I shall teach there Apport Gelidia and Reditus Gelidia. Seek out Cold Peak to the north west, as they will only work there."=ZH=“我會在那裡教阿普特·吉利迪亞和雷德圖斯·吉利迪亞。尋找西北方向的冷峰，因為他們只能在那裡工作。”
"Bring Lanatir with you, or, if you cannot convince him to come, coax from him the Wand of Power and the Sphere of Lanatir."=ZH=“帶上拉納提爾，或者，如果你不能說服他來，就從他那裡得到力量魔杖和拉納提爾球體。”
Seek out Alliria in Lucencia. The Crystal Spring in the east offers passage to Lucencia using Apport Lucencia. Recover the Crown of Truth and the Belt of Alliria!"=ZH=尋找盧森西亞的艾莉亞。東部的水晶泉 (Crystal Spring) 提供前往盧森西亞 (Lucencia) 的通道。奪回真理之冠和艾莉莉亞腰帶！ 」
The old man appears visibly shaken by the news of Lanatir's death. After a long silence he stares off into space and mumbles, "Is it to be that way then?"=ZH=得知拉納提爾的死訊，老人顯然感到震驚。沉默良久後，他凝視著太空，咕噥道：“就這樣嗎？”
His eyes focus upon you again, and a grim look washes over his features. "Now you are bound for Lucencia."=ZH=他的目光再次集中在你身上，臉上浮現出嚴峻的表情。 “現在你要去盧森西亞了。”
"To this end, I will teach thee Apport Lucencia and Reditus Lucencia which you must use at the Crystal Spring to the north east."=ZH=“為此，我將教你阿普特·盧森西亞和雷迪圖斯·盧森西亞，你必須在東北方的水晶泉中使用它們。”
"I dare not hope Alliria lives, so I want you to recover the Crown of Truth and the Belt of Alliria. Beware, for she had a jealous consort, and he will protect her as best he can."=ZH=“我不敢希望艾莉莉亞活著，所以我希望你奪回真理之冠和艾莉莉亞腰帶。小心，因為她有一個嫉妒的配偶，他會盡力保護她。”
"Get thee to Kinestia and recover The Hammer of Wrath and Ferofist's Helm. Your path begins in the Dwarven Mines to the south west!"=ZH=“帶你前往基內斯蒂亞並找回憤怒之鎚和費洛菲力量的頭盔。你的道路從西南邊的矮人礦井開始！”
The old man's look of grim determination withstands news of Alliria's death, but the information saps some of his strength.=ZH=老人一臉冷酷的決心，承受艾莉莉亞死亡的消息，但這個消息削弱了他的一些力量。
"Quickly then, my children, to distant Kinestia!"=ZH=“那麼，我的孩子們，快去遙遠的動力學吧！”
"Learn now, Apport Kinestia and Reditus Kinestia, which will take you to Ferofist in Kinestia if cast from the Dwarven Mines in the south west."=ZH=“現在學習，Appport Kinestia 和 Reditus Kinestia，如果從西南部的矮人礦山施放，它將帶你到 Kinestia 的 Ferofist。”
"You must return here with The Hammer of Wrath and Ferofist's Helm. I know Ferofist yet lives, but I cannot be sure of how long he will survive."=ZH=「你必須帶著憤怒之鎚和費羅菲力量的頭盔返回這裡。我知道費羅菲力量還活著，但我不能確定他還能活多久。”
"Hurry, the pace quickens, and the outlook is horrible if you fail!"=ZH=「快點，速度加快，失敗的話語後果不堪設想！」
"Get to Tenebrosia! Bring me the Helm of Justice and Sceadu's Cloak. Seek the Shadow Rock!"=ZH=“前往特內布羅西亞！給我帶來正義頭盔和斯卡杜斗篷。尋找暗影岩石！”
The old man's eyes narrow as you tell of Ferofist's end. "An alliance with the dark one, did he say? It is well he realized his folly in the end."=ZH=當你講述費羅菲力量的結局時，老人的眼睛瞇了起來。 “他是說與黑暗勢力結盟嗎？還好他最終意識到了自己的愚蠢。”
He pauses a second, and you see utter weariness send a tremor through him. "Now to Tenebrosia for all of you."=ZH=他停頓了一下，你看到他全身顫抖，極度疲倦。 “現在為你們所有人前往特內布羅西亞。”
I endow thee with the spells to travel there and return: Apport Tenebrosia and Reditus Tenebrosia. The spell must be cast at Shadow Rock, which can be seen towering in the east."=ZH=我賦予你前往那裡並返回的咒語：阿普特特內布羅西亞和雷迪圖力量內布羅西亞。這個咒語必須在暗影岩施展，暗影岩在東方高聳入雲。 」
"Remember, in the land where night is day and day is night, nothing is as you know it to be. Paradox lives there - trust no one but yourselves."=ZH=「記住，在黑夜就是白天、白天就是黑夜的土地上，一切都不是你所知道的那樣。悖論就在那裡——除了你自己，不要相信任何人。 」
"From there I require the Helm of Justice and Sceadu's Cloak."=ZH=「從那裡我需要正義頭盔和斯卡杜斗篷。 」
"Off with you to Tarmitia, land of unceasing warfare. From the Vale to the South, thou must cast Apport Tarmitia. Recover Werra's Shield and the Strifespear."=ZH=「帶你前往塔爾米提亞，那片戰火不斷的土地。從山谷到南方，你必須施放塔米提亞。找回維拉的盾牌和衝突之矛。 」
"No!" the old man cries out, "not Sceadu as well. The dark one is utterly mad. Still," the old man smiles, "he burns the chaff while I sow the seed."=ZH=「不！ 」老人喊道：「斯卡杜也不是。黑暗之人徹底瘋狂了。儘管如此，」老人微笑著說，「他燒掉了糠秕，而我則播種了。 」
"Off with you to Tarmitia, the land of unceasing warfare. A myriad of ages meld together there, and your success depends upon your ability to adapt."=ZH=「帶你前往塔爾米提亞，那片戰火不斷的土地。無數的時代在那裡融合在一起，你的成功取決於你的適應能力。 」

The old man lies dead. His last request still plagues your memories. You must endure stench to get to Malefia and help Hawkslayer to defeat the Mad God Tarjan before he destroys all of reality.=ZH=老人已經死了。他最後的請求仍然困擾著你的記憶。你必須忍受惡臭才能到達馬勒菲亞，並幫助屠鷹者在狂神塔爾詹摧毀所有現實之前擊敗他。
"I know," the old man gurgles, "Werra lies dead."\n\nBlood bubbles up on his lips and joins the dark stains on the rest of his clothing. "I, too, have been slain by the Mad One."=ZH=「我知道，」老人咯咯笑道，「維拉已經死了。」\n\n血從他的嘴唇上冒出來，與他衣服上其他黑色的污跡融為一體。 “我也被瘋子殺了。”

The game will pause and bring up the Pause Menu whenever the game window loses focus.=ZH=每當遊戲視窗失去焦點時，遊戲就會暫停並顯示暫停選單。
Mouse look in the 3d view is reversed on the Y axis.=ZH=3D 視圖中的滑鼠外觀在 Y 軸上是相反的。
This will use an alternative layout where the 3d view is much larger, but the player info and message scroll are smaller and use smaller text.\nNote: This is NOT supported on 4:3 aspect.=ZH=這將使用另一種佈局，其中 3D 視圖更大，但播放器資訊和訊息滾動更小，並且使用更小的文字。 \n注意：4:3 寬高比不支援此功能。
Combat actions that are not available will be transparent, rather than being removed. This way the options do not move on screen between characters and are easier to click on.=ZH=不可用的戰鬥動作將是透明的，而不是被刪除。這樣，選項就不會在螢幕上的字元之間移動，並且更容易單擊。
For AZERTY keyboard users, you can now navigate the game using ESDF keys, instead of the usual WASD keys.=ZH=對於 AZERTY 鍵盤用戶，您現在可以使用 ESDF 鍵來導航遊戲，而不是通常的 WASD 鍵。
Do a visual effect whenever a monster or ally is attacked in combat. You may prefer to turn this off if you want a more passive experience.=ZH=每當怪物或盟友在戰鬥中受到攻擊時就會產生視覺效果。如果您想要更被動的體驗，您可能更願意關閉此功能。
Place a separator between character inventory and party inventory. This is more intuitive, though it means the UI must rebuild when items are equipped and unequipped.=ZH=在角色庫存和隊伍庫存之間放置分隔符號。這更直觀，儘管這意味著在裝備和未裝備物品時必須重建 UI。
Toggles Fullscreen. Fullscreen maximises the game window to fit the screen.=ZH=切換全螢幕。全螢幕最大化遊戲視窗以適合螢幕。
Toggles SSAO. SSAO (Screen Space Ambient Occlusion) creates advanced lighting effects for each point and how exposed it is to ambient lighting. Turning SSAO off may improve performance.=ZH=切換 SSAO。 SSAO（螢幕空間環境光遮蔽）為每個點及其暴露於環境照明的程度創造高級照明效果。關閉 SSAO 可能會提高效能。
Toggles Shadows. Cast shadows from sunlight or moonlight.=ZH=切換陰影。從陽光或月光投射陰影。
Toggles Bloom. Bloom produces edges of light extending from the borders of bright areas, making lights brighter than what they actually appear.=ZH=切換餘暉效果。「餘暉設定」會產生從明亮區域的邊界延伸出來的光線邊緣，讓光線比實際看起來更亮。
Toggles Grass. Grass is nature's carpet.=ZH=切換草地效果。草是大自然的地毯。
Adjusts in game brightness levels.=ZH=調整遊戲亮度等級。
Text scroll speed changes the speed that scroll text in combat gets presented and shifted up. Scroll speed can also be adjusted by its <l="MAN_COMBAT_COMMANDS","shortcut keys"/>.=ZH=文字滾動速度會改變戰鬥中滾動文字呈現和向上移動的速度。滾動速度也可以透過其<l="MAN_COMBAT_COMMANDS","shortcut keys"/>來調整。
Minimap alpha controls the visibility of the minimap in the <l="MAN_3D_GAME_VIEW","3D game view"/>.=ZH=小地圖 alpha 控制小地圖在<l="MAN_3D_GAME_VIEW","3D game view"/>中的可見度。

Bard's Tale Trilogy Remaster Manual=ZH=冰城傳奇三部曲重製手冊
About=ZH=關於
The Bard’s Tale was originally written as trilogy of games. The release of the 3 titles was between 1985-1988.\n\n<color=@#Heading#>\n\n<b>Tales of the Unknown (BT1)</b>\n\n<b>The Destiny Knight (BT2)</b>\n\n<b>Thief of Fate (BT3)</b>\n\n</color>\n\nThis remaster combines the three games into a single narrative. Some features from The Destiny Knight and Thief of Fate have been taken through all three games to provide a consistent gameplay experience across the 3 volumes.=ZH=《冰城傳奇》最初是作為遊戲三部曲編寫的。這 3 部作品的發行時間為 1985 年至 1988 年。<color=@#Heading#><b>未知故事 (BT1)</b><b>命運騎士 (BT2)</b><b>命運小偷 (BT3)</b></color>此重製版將三款遊戲合併為一個敘述。 《命運騎士》和《命運之賊》的一些功能已在所有三款遊戲中採用，以在三卷中提供一致的遊戲體驗。

3D Game View=ZH=3D遊戲視圖
<pos=15%><sprite=51/></pos><line-height=1500%>\n\n</line-height>\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>3D View</b></color>\n\nLocated in the center, framed by big stone blocks, this is the window which shows the 3D world that the party is exploring. All character, non-player-character cameos and visual effects will appear in this window. The cursor can be used inside of this window to <l="MAN_MOVEMENT","move"/> the party.\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>Magic Detection Skull</b></color>\n\nLocated in the middle of the top of the stone frame is the <l="MAN_MAGIC_DETECTION_SKULL","skull"/>. It changes its appearance to reflect the magic it is sensing. It helps track important information which is blessing or cursing the party.\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>Gold & Inventory</b></color>\n\nGold and Inventory space are displayed along the bottom of the 3D window frame.\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>Text Plate</b></color>\n\nAt the bottom of the 3D view is a black banner that details a number of things. Such as what location the party is currently in, what person or object is the focus and names of enemies and group size the party is facing. This text is changing quite frequently.\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>Minimap</b></color>\n\nThe minimap is located in the bottom right corner of the 3D view (<l="MAN_ACTIONS","when active"/>). The minimap is a small map that assists navigation, like the one found in the journal. The orientation of the minimap and the player is subject to change based on the active spells and effects.\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>Left Ribbon Action Buttons</b></color>\n\nThis ribbon in the top left holds the buttons for the alternative panels -<line-height=130%>\n\n</line-height><sprite=10/> <b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>F10</color></b> - Opens the Menu.\n\n\n\n<sprite=11/> <b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>F1</color></b> - Opens this Manual.\n\n\n\n<sprite=12/> <b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>F2</color></b> - Opens the Cloth Map.\n\n\n\n<sprite=13/> <b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>F3</color></b> - Opens the Bestiary.\n\n\n\n<sprite=14/> <b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>F4</color></b> - Opens Game Stats.\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>Right Ribbon Action Buttons</b></color>\n\nThis ribbon in the top right holds the buttons for actions -<line-height=130%>\n\n</line-height><sprite=9/> - More Options.\n\n\n\n<sprite=15/> @#BUTTON_WhereAmI#.\n\n\n\n<sprite=16/> @#BUTTON_CastSpell#.\n\n\n\n<sprite=17/> @#BUTTON_PlaySong#.\n\n\n\n<sprite=18/> @#BUTTON_UseItem#. \n\n\n\n<sprite=19/> @#BUTTON_PortalUp#.\n\n\n\n<sprite=20/> @#BUTTON_PortalDown#.\n\n\n\n<sprite=21/> @#BUTTON_PartyAttack#.\n\n\n\n<sprite=22/> @#BUTTON_PartyOrder#.\n\n\n\n<sprite=23/> @#BUTTON_ToggleMinimap#.\n\n\n\n<sprite=24/> @#BUTTON_ToggleJournal#.=ZH=<pos=15%><sprite=51/></pos><line-height=1500%></line-height><color=@#SubHeading#><b>3D視圖</b></color>位於中心，由大石塊包圍，這是展示小隊正在探索的3D世界的窗口。所有角色、非玩家角色客串和視覺效果都會出現在該視窗中。可以在該視窗內使用遊標來<l="MAN_MOVEMENT","move"/>方。<color=@#SubHeading#><b>魔法探測頭骨</b></color>位於石框頂部中間的是<l="MAN_MAGIC_DETECTION_SKULL","skull"/>。它改變自己的外觀以反映它所感知的魔法。它有助於追蹤祝福或詛咒隊伍的重要訊息。<color=@#SubHeading#><b>金幣和庫存</b></color>金幣和庫存空間沿著 3D 視窗框架的底部顯示。<color=@#SubHeading#><b>文字板</b></color>3D 視圖的底部是一個黑色橫幅，詳細說明了許多內容。例如隊伍目前位於什麼位置、焦點是什麼人或物體、敵人的名字、隊伍規模。此文字變更非常頻繁。<color=@#SubHeading#><b>小圖</b></color>小地圖位於 3D 視圖 (<l="MAN_ACTIONS","when active"/>) 的右下角。小地圖是一種輔助導航的小地圖，就像日記中的地圖一樣。小地圖和玩家的方向可能會根據啟動的法術和效果而改變。<color=@#SubHeading#><b>左側功能區操作按鈕</b></color>左上角的此功能區包含備用面板的按鈕 -<line-height=130%></line-height><sprite=10/><b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>F10</color></b>- 開啟選單。<sprite=11/><b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>F1</color></b>- 開啟本手冊。<sprite=12/><b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>F2</color></b>- 打開布料貼圖。<sprite=13/><b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>F3</color></b>- 打開動物寓言。<sprite=14/><b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>F4</color></b>- 開啟遊戲統計資料。<color=@#SubHeading#><b>右側功能區操作按鈕</b></color>右上角的此功能區包含操作按鈕 -<line-height=130%></line-height><sprite=9/>- 更多選項。<sprite=15/>@#BUTTON_WhereAmI#。<sprite=16/>@#BUTTON_CastSpell#。<sprite=17/>@#BUTTON_PlaySong#。<sprite=18/>@#BUTTON_UseItem#。<sprite=19/>@#BUTTON_PortalUp#。<sprite=20/>@#BUTTON_PortalDown#。<sprite=21/>@#BUTTON_PartyAttack#。<sprite=22/>@#BUTTON_PartyOrder#。<sprite=23/>@#BUTTON_ToggleMinimap#。<sprite=24/>@#BUTTON_ToggleJournal#。
Scroll=ZH=捲動
<pos=50%><sprite=34/></pos><line-height=1250%>\n\n</line-height>This scroll contains essentially all of the changing text within the game, where majority of the in game information is read and most in game actions are decided. Information such as - Character information, Inventory and Items management, Combat text, Choices and Commands, Spell casting and even a text field for typing.\n\n\n\nAll of the options presented in the scroll can be clicked with mouse or the appropriate <color=@#InteractionCommand#>key</color> pressed on a keyboard.\n\n\n\n@#MOUSE_SCROLL_REVIEW#=ZH=<pos=50%><sprite=34/></pos><line-height=1250%></line-height>該捲軸基本上包含遊戲中所有變化的文本，其中讀取大部分遊戲資訊並決定大部分遊戲動作。諸如角色資訊、庫存和物品管理、戰鬥文字、選擇和命令、拼字甚至用於輸入的文字欄位等資訊。 \n \n \n \n 捲動中顯示的所有選項都可以用滑鼠或鍵盤上對應的<color=@#InteractionCommand#>鍵</color>點選。 \n \n \n \n @#MOUSE_SCROLL_REVIEW#
Party Status=ZH=聚會狀態
The party status contains all of the information about the party, this includes -\n\nParty slots and party slot numbers, all party members and their names, party member <l="MAN_ARMOR_CLASS",@#AC#/>, Settings=ZH=設定
Multiple adjustments can be made via the settings panel in the pause menu.<line-height=130%>\n\n</line-height><sprite=10/> <b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>F10</color></b><line-height=140%>\n\n</line-height><l="MAN_GRAPHICS"/>\n\n<l="MAN_AUDIO"/>\n\n<l="MAN_GAMEPLAY"/>=ZH=可以透過暫停選單中的設定面板進行多項調整。<line-height=130%></line-height><sprite=10/><b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>F10</color></b><line-height=140%></line-height><l="MAN_GRAPHICS"/><l="MAN_AUDIO"/><l="MAN_GAMEPLAY"/>
Audio=ZH=音訊
Audio Settings.\n\n\n\n<sprite=57/> Adjust the volume of all music in the game.\n\n\n\n\n\n<sprite=58/> Adjust the volume of all sound effects (SFX) in the game.=ZH=音頻設定。<sprite=57/>會調整遊戲中所有音樂的音量。<sprite=58/>調節遊戲中所有音效（SFX）的音量。
Controls=ZH=控制
The Bard's Tale Trilogy presents choices throughout the game. Choices are <b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>highlighted</color></b> in yellow and defines what key to press to select that choice.\n\n<pos=1%><sprite=25/></pos>\n\n<margin=6.25fem>The cursor can also be used to click any of the same options and buttons throughout The Bard's Tale Trilogy.</margin>=ZH=冰城傳奇三部曲在整個遊戲中提供了選擇。選項是<b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>以黃色突出顯示</color></b>，並定義按哪個鍵來選擇該選項。<pos=1%><sprite=25/></pos><margin=6.25fem>遊標也可用於點擊《冰城傳奇三部曲》中的任何相同選項和按鈕。</margin>
Movement=ZH=運動
<color=@#SubHeading#><b>Keyboard</b></color>\n\n<b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>W</color></b> - move forward.\n\n<b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>S</color></b> - turn to face the opposite direction.\n\n<b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>A</color></b> - turn left.\n\n<b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>D</color></b> - turn right.\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>Mouse</b></color>\n\nWhen the pointer is placed within the <l="MAN_3D_GAME_VIEW","3D game view"/>, it turns into a directional arrow. As the cursor is moved, the arrow points out the various directions of can travel. Ahead, around corners, into buildings, through doors. Click to go in the direction currently indicated by the arrow.\n\n\n\n<pos=40%><sprite=59/></pos>\n\n<pos=31%><sprite=60/></pos><pos=48%><sprite=61/></pos>\n\n<pos=40%><sprite=62/></pos><line-height=200%>\n\n</line-height><b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>Up-Arrow</color></b> - move forward.\n\n<b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>Down-Arrow</color></b> - turn around 180 degrees.\n\n<b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>Left-Arrow</color></b> - turn left 90 degrees.\n\n<b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>Right-Arrow</color></b> - turn right 90 degrees.\n\nThe left mouse button can also be held down to continuously move.=ZH=<color=@#SubHeading#><b>鍵盤</b></color><b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>W</color></b>- 向前移動。<b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>S</color></b>- 轉向相反方向。<b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>A</color></b>- 左轉。<b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>D</color></b>- 右轉。<color=@#SubHeading#><b>老鼠</b></color>當指標置於<l="MAN_3D_GAME_VIEW","3D game view"/>內時，它會變成方向箭頭。當遊標移動時，箭頭會指出可以行進的各個方向。向前，繞過角落，進入建築物，穿過門。按一下可沿目前箭頭指示的方向前進。<pos=40%><sprite=59/></pos><pos=31%><sprite=60/></pos><pos=48%><sprite=61/></pos><pos=40%><sprite=62/></pos><line-height=200%></line-height><b><color=@#InteractionCommand#><sprite=62/></pos><line-height=200%></line-height><b><color=@#InteractionCommand#><sprite=62/></color><line-height=200%></line-height><b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>向上箭頭</color>Y</b>1YYY</b>向前移動。<b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>下箭頭</color></b>- 旋轉 180 度。<b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>左箭頭</color></b>- 左轉 90 度。<b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>右箭頭</color></b>- 右轉 90 度。 \n \n 按住滑鼠左鍵也可以連續移動。
Actions=ZH=行動
Clicking a character name plate in <l="MAN_PARTY_STATUS","party status"/> or pressing (<b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>1</color></b> - <b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>7</color></b>) opens character information in the <l="MAN_SCROLL","scroll"/>.<line-height=130%>\n\n</line-height><sprite=10/>  <b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>F10</color></b> - Opens the Menu.\n\n\n\n<sprite=11/>  <b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>F1</color></b> - Opens this Manual.\n\n\n\n<sprite=12/>  <b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>F2</color></b> - Opens the Cloth Map.\n\n\n\n<sprite=13/>  <b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>F3</color></b> - Opens the Bestiary.\n\n\n\n<sprite=14/>  <b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>F4</color></b> - Opens Game Stats.\n\n\n\n<sprite=9/> - More Options.<line-height=130%>\n\n</line-height><indent=1.2f%>When peacefully exploring streets, cities or navigating through dungeons the more options button can be pressed to use the following buttons -</indent>\n\n\n\n<line-height=130%><sprite=15/> @#BUTTON_WhereAmI#.\n\n</line-height><indent=1.2f%>Learn the name of the location the party is in, and the time of day. If within a city this will also tell the street name.</indent>\n\n\n\n<line-height=130%><sprite=16/> @#BUTTON_CastSpell#.\n\n</line-height><indent=1.2f%>Casts a spell from a selected mage. This can also be done by pressing a magic users party slot number (<b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>1</color></b> - <b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>7</color></b>) key, or click on a magic user's name in the party status and press (<b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>S</color></b>) - to open the spell list.</indent>\n\n\n\n<line-height=130%><sprite=17/> @#BUTTON_PlaySong#.\n\n</line-height><indent=1.2f%>Sing a Bard's tune.</indent>\n\n\n\n<line-height=130%><sprite=18/> @#BUTTON_UseItem#. \n\n</line-height><indent=1.2f%>Use an equipped item.</indent>\n\n\n\n<line-height=130%><sprite=19/> @#BUTTON_PortalUp#.\n\n</line-height><indent=1.2f%>Ascend <u>up</u> through a portal.</indent>\n\n\n\n<line-height=130%><sprite=20/> @#BUTTON_PortalDown#.\n\n</line-height><indent=1.2f%>Descend <u>down</u> through a portal.</indent>\n\n\n\n<line-height=130%><sprite=21/> @#BUTTON_PartyAttack#.\n\n</line-height><indent=1.2f%>Assault another member of the party, including special members.</indent>\n\n\n\n<line-height=130%><sprite=22/> @#BUTTON_PartyOrder#.\n\n</line-height><indent=1.2f%>Set a new party order from (<b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>1</color></b> - <b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>7</color></b>). This can also be done by dragging and dropping character names in the party status.</size></indent>\n\n\n\n<line-height=130%><sprite=23/> @#BUTTON_ToggleMinimap#.\n\n</line-height><indent=1.2f%>Toggles the minimap in the 3d view.</indent>\n\n\n\n<line-height=130%><sprite=24/> @#BUTTON_ToggleJournal#.\n\n</line-height><indent=1.2f%>Opens the journal.</indent>=ZH=點選<l="MAN_PARTY_STATUS","party status"/>中的角色名牌或按(<b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>1</color></b>-<b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>7</color></b>) 可開啟<l="MAN_SCROLL","scroll"/>中的角色資訊。<line-height=130%></line-height><sprite=10/><b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>F10</color></b>- 開啟選單。<sprite=11/><b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>F1</color></b>- 開啟本手冊。<sprite=12/><b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>F2</color></b>- 打開布料貼圖。<sprite=13/><b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>F3</color></b>- 開啟寓言集。<sprite=14/><b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>F4</color></b>- 開啟遊戲統計資料。<sprite=9/>- 更多選項。<line-height=130%></line-height><indent=1.2f%>當平靜地探索街道、城市或穿越地下城時，可以按下更多選項按鈕以使用以下按鈕
