(<b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>1</color></b>-<b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>7</color></b>) 設定新的聚會順序。這也可以透過在隊伍狀態中拖放角色名稱來完成。</size></indent><line-height=130%><sprite=23/>@#BUTTON_ToggleMinimap#。</line-height><indent=1.2f%>在 3d 視圖中切換小地圖。</indent><line-height=130%><sprite=24/>@#BUTTON_ToggleJournal#。</line-height><indent=1.2f%>開啟日誌。</indent>
Combat=ZH=戰鬥
During battle, the actions and options characters can take appear in the <l="MAN_SCROLL","scroll"/>.\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#>@#Advance ahead#</color> - Moves the party closer to the nearest group of enemies.\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#>@#Fight bravely#</color> - Engages in combat.\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#>@#Run away#</color> - Attempts to try and safely escape combat without having to engage.\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#>@#Attack#</color> - Physically assaults foes any of the first 4 groups of monsters within 10'.\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#>@#Defend#</color> - Do not interact this round - reduces chance of being hit.\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#>@#Bard song#</color> - Plays a bard song to affect the party or groups of foes.\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#>@#Hide in the shadows#</color> - Try to avoid combat by hiding. If successful, will not be a target of attack in the upcoming combat round.\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#>@#Cast a spell#</color> - Casts a spell at enemies or the party.\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#>@#Use an equipped item#</color> - Use an item or currently equipped item.\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#>@#Ranged attack#</color> - Shoot or throw a projectile from a distance.\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#>@#Party attack#</color> - Assault another member of the party, including special members. \n\n<color=@#SubHeading#>@#EQUIP_OR_UNEQUIP#</color> - The party-member will equip or unequip a single item.\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#>@#KICK_DEAD_MONSTERS#</color> - Free up character slots during combat by removing all existing dead monsters from the party.\n\n\n\n<sprite=26/> - increase message scrolling rate.\n\n<sprite=27/> - decrease message scrolling rate.=ZH=在戰鬥中，角色可以採取的行動和選項出現在<l="MAN_SCROLL","scroll"/>中。<color=@#SubHeading#>@#前進#</color>- 讓隊伍更靠近最近的敵人群。<color=@#SubHeading#>@#勇敢戰鬥#</color>- 參與戰鬥。<color=@#SubHeading#>@#逃跑#</color>- 嘗試在不參與戰鬥的情況下安全地逃離戰鬥。<color=@#SubHeading#>@#Attack#</color>- 對 10 英尺內前 4 組怪物中的任何一個進行物理攻擊。<color=@#SubHeading#>@#Defend#</color>- 本輪不要互動 - 減少被擊中的機會。<color=@#SubHeading#>@#吟遊詩人歌曲#</color>- 播放吟遊詩人歌曲來影響隊伍或敵人群體。<color=@#SubHeading#>@#潛伏於陰影#</color>- 嘗試透過隱藏來避免戰鬥。如果成功，將不會成為下一輪戰鬥的攻擊目標。<color=@#SubHeading#>@#施法#</color>- 對敵人或隊伍施法。<color=@#SubHeading#>@#使用裝備的物品#</color>- 使用物品或當前裝備的物品。<color=@#SubHeading#>@#遠程攻擊#</color>- 從遠處射擊或投擲彈。<color=@#SubHeading#>@#隊伍攻擊#</color>- 攻擊隊伍的另一位成員，包括特殊成員。<color=@#SubHeading#>@#EQUIP_OR_UNEQUIP#</color>- 隊伍成員將裝備或取消裝備單一物品。<color=@#SubHeading#>@#KICK_DEAD_MONSTERS#</color>- 透過從隊伍中移除所有現有的死亡怪物，在戰鬥中釋放角色位置。<sprite=26/>- 增加訊息滾動率。<sprite=27/>- 降低訊息滾動速率。
Character=ZH=性格
Characters can be created at the <b><l="MAN_ADVENTURERS_GUILD","Adventurer's Guild"/></b> in the\n\n<b>Tales of the Unknown (BT1)</b>, <b>The Destiny Knight (BT2)</b>, and in the <b><l="MAN_REFUGEE_CAMP","refugee camp"/></b> in the <b>Thief of Fate (BT3)</b>.=ZH=可以在<b>未知故事 (BT1)</b>、<b>命運騎士 (BT2)</b>中的<b><l="MAN_ADVENTURERS_GUILD","Adventurer's Guild"/></b>以及<b>命運之盜 (BT3)</b>中的<b><l="MAN_REFUGEE_CAMP","refugee camp"/></b>中創建角色。
Race=ZH=競賽
Humans are not the only race in the world of The Bard's Tale. There is no single best race, each has its own strengths. Some are more magically inclined, some are stronger and some are smarter. There are 7 races:\n\n<b><l="MAN_HUMAN"/></b>\n\n<b><l="MAN_ELF"/></b>\n\n<b><l="MAN_DWARF"/></b>\n\n<b><l="MAN_HOBBIT"/></b>\n\n<b><l="MAN_HALFELF"/></b>\n\n<b><l="MAN_HALFORC"/></b>\n\n<b><l="MAN_GNOME"/></b>=ZH=人類並不是《冰城傳奇》世界中唯一的種族。沒有單一的最佳比賽，每場比賽都有自己的優勢。有些人更傾向於魔法，有些人更強大，有些人更聰明。有 7 場：<b><l="MAN_HUMAN"/></b><b><l="MAN_ELF"/></b><b><l="MAN_DWARF"/></b><b><l="MAN_HOBBIT"/></b><b><l="MAN_HALFELF"/></b><b><l="MAN_HALFORC"/>NN
Human=ZH=人類
While possibly being of hardier stock, this character is nonetheless like you or me. The people of this race are known for their inventiveness, hardiness, aggressiveness, and downright stubbornness in the face of adversity.\n\n\n\nHuman capable classes -\n\n<l="MAN_WARRIOR"/>\n\n<l="MAN_PALADIN"/>\n\n<l="MAN_ROGUE"/>\n\n<l="MAN_BARD"/>\n\n<l="MAN_HUNTER"/>\n\n<l="MAN_MONK"/>\n\n<l="MAN_CONJURER"/>\n\n<l="MAN_MAGICIAN"/>=ZH=雖然這個角色可能比較堅強，但它仍然像你或我。這個種族的人民以其創造力、堅韌、進取心和麵對逆境時的頑強而聞名。 \n \n \n \n 人類能力類別 -<l="MAN_WARRIOR"/><l="MAN_PALADIN"/><l="MAN_ROGUE"/><l="MAN_BARD"/><l="MAN_HUNTER"/><l="MAN_MONK"/><l="MAN_CONJURER"/><l="MAN_MAGICIAN"/>
Elf=ZH=精靈
These fair-haired, fair-skinned people closely resemble humans but are frequently taller, faster, and more agile due to their slim builds. The Elven people are highly skilled in the arts, especially magic and war.\n\n\n\nElf capable classes -\n\n<l="MAN_WARRIOR"/>\n\n<l="MAN_PALADIN"/>\n\n<l="MAN_ROGUE"/>\n\n<l="MAN_BARD"/>\n\n<l="MAN_MONK"/>\n\n<l="MAN_CONJURER"/>\n\n<l="MAN_MAGICIAN"/>\n\n\n\nNot capable classes -\n\n<l="MAN_HUNTER"/>=ZH=這些金髮、皮膚白皙的人與人類非常相似，但由於身材苗條，通常更高、更快、更敏捷。精靈族精通藝術，尤其是魔法和戰爭。 \n \n \n \n 精靈能力類別 -<l="MAN_WARRIOR"/><l="MAN_PALADIN"/><l="MAN_ROGUE"/><l="MAN_BARD"/><l="MAN_MONK"/><l="MAN_CONJURER"/><l="MAN_MAGICIAN"/>不具備能力分類 -<l="MAN_HUNTER"/>
Dwarf=ZH=矮人
The Dwarven people are short and stout, extremely strong and healthy, but not amazingly intelligent. What they lack in intelligence, they more than make up for in their fighting abilities.\n\n\n\nDwarf capable classes -\n\n<l="MAN_WARRIOR"/>\n\n<l="MAN_PALADIN"/>\n\n<l="MAN_ROGUE"/>\n\n<l="MAN_BARD"/>\n\n<l="MAN_HUNTER"/>\n\n<l="MAN_MONK"/>\n\n\n\nNot capable classes -\n\n<l="MAN_CONJURER"/>\n\n<l="MAN_MAGICIAN"/>=ZH=矮人人矮小而粗壯，極其強壯和健康，但智力並不驚人。他們智力上的不足，可以用戰鬥能力來彌補。 \n \n \n \n 矮人能力類別 -<l="MAN_WARRIOR"/><l="MAN_PALADIN"/><l="MAN_ROGUE"/><l="MAN_BARD"/><l="MAN_HUNTER"/><l="MAN_MONK"/>不具備能力類別 -<l="MAN_CONJURER"/><l="MAN_MAGICIAN"/>
Hobbit=ZH=哈比人
Hobbits are slightly smaller than Dwarves but are wise, nimble, dexterous and usually very patient. It is said, if need be, a hobbit can steal the gold-purse from a shadow. Just the right makeup for a rogue.\n\n\n\nHobbit capable classes -\n\n<l="MAN_WARRIOR"/>\n\n<l="MAN_ROGUE"/>\n\n<l="MAN_BARD"/>\n\n<l="MAN_MONK"/>\n\n<l="MAN_CONJURER"/>\n\n<l="MAN_MAGICIAN"/>\n\n\n\nNot capable classes -\n\n<l="MAN_PALADIN"/>\n\n<l="MAN_HUNTER"/>=ZH=哈比人比矮人稍小，但聰明、敏捷、靈巧，通常非常有耐心。據說，如果需要的話，哈比人可以從陰影中偷走金錢包。剛好適合盜賊的角色。 \n \n \n \n 哈比人能力類別 -<l="MAN_WARRIOR"/><l="MAN_ROGUE"/><l="MAN_BARD"/><l="MAN_MONK"/><l="MAN_CONJURER"/><l="MAN_MAGICIAN"/>不具備能力類別 -<l="MAN_PALADIN"/><l="MAN_HUNTER"/>
Half-Elf=ZH=半精靈
These people are living proof of the compatibility between humans and elves. They are fair-haired and light-skinned like elves, but gain some bulk and strength through their human ancestry.\n\n\n\nHalf-Elf capable classes -\n\n<l="MAN_WARRIOR"/>\n\n<l="MAN_ROGUE"/>\n\n<l="MAN_BARD"/>\n\n<l="MAN_MONK"/>\n\n<l="MAN_CONJURER"/>\n\n<l="MAN_MAGICIAN"/>\n\n\n\nNot capable classes -\n\n<l="MAN_PALADIN"/>\n\n<l="MAN_HUNTER"/>=ZH=這些人就是人類和精靈之間和諧相處的活生生的證明。他們像精靈一樣有著金髮和淺色皮膚，但由於他們的人類血統而獲得了一些體積和力量。 \n \n \n \n 半精靈能力分類 -<l="MAN_WARRIOR"/><l="MAN_ROGUE"/><l="MAN_BARD"/><l="MAN_MONK"/><l="MAN_CONJURER"/><l="MAN_MAGICIAN"/>不具備能力類別 -<l="MAN_PALADIN"/><l="MAN_HUNTER"/>
Half-Orc=ZH=半獸人
Orcs are large, semi-intelligent, pig-like creatures that can usually be found working for evil wizards. Half-orcs, being half human, aren't quite as despicable as full orcs but you wouldn't want to date one.\n\n\n\nHalf-Orc capable classes -\n\n<l="MAN_WARRIOR"/>\n\n<l="MAN_ROGUE"/>\n\n<l="MAN_HUNTER"/>\n\n<l="MAN_CONJURER"/>\n\n<l="MAN_MAGICIAN"/>\n\n\n\nNot capable classes -\n\n<l="MAN_PALADIN"/>\n\n<l="MAN_BARD"/>\n\n<l="MAN_MONK"/>=ZH=獸人是體型巨大、半智能、像豬的生物，通常為邪惡巫師工作。半獸人是半人類，不像全獸人那麼卑鄙，但你不會想和他們約會。 \n \n \n \n 半獸人有能力的職業 -<l="MAN_WARRIOR"/><l="MAN_ROGUE"/><l="MAN_HUNTER"/><l="MAN_CONJURER"/><l="MAN_MAGICIAN"/>無能的職業 -<l="MAN_PALADIN"/><l="MAN_BARD"/><l="MAN_MONK"/>
Gnome=ZH=侏儒
These people resemble dwarves, but usually have less hair, shorter tempers, and are always better at using magic.\n\n\n\nGnome capable classes -\n\n<l="MAN_WARRIOR"/>\n\n<l="MAN_ROGUE"/>\n\n<l="MAN_HUNTER"/>\n\n<l="MAN_MONK"/>\n\n<l="MAN_CONJURER"/>\n\n<l="MAN_MAGICIAN"/>\n\n\n\nNot capable classes -\n\n<l="MAN_PALADIN"/>\n\n<l="MAN_BARD"/>=ZH=這些人與矮人相似，但通常頭髮較少，脾氣也較矮，並且總是更擅長使用魔法。 \n \n \n \n Gnome 可用類別 -<l="MAN_WARRIOR"/><l="MAN_ROGUE"/><l="MAN_HUNTER"/><l="MAN_MONK"/><l="MAN_CONJURER"/><l="MAN_MAGICIAN"/>不可用類別 -<l="MAN_PALADIN"/><l="MAN_BARD"/>
Class=ZH=班級
Classes represent a characters profession. They determine what gear they can were and use and what, if any, magic they can wield.=ZH=職業代表角色的職業。他們決定他們可以使用什麼裝備，以及他們可以使用什麼魔法（如果有的話）。
Fighters=ZH=戰士
Fighters are classes best suited to physical combat.=ZH=戰士是最適合肉搏的職業。
Warrior=ZH=戰士
<color=@#Fighter#>Fighter</color>\n\nWarriors are fighters. They can use nearly every weapon there is. For every 4 levels of experience after the first, warriors get an <l="MAN_MELEE_COMBAT","extra attack"/> capability in combat.\n\n\n\nCapable of becoming a <l="MAN_GEOMANCER"/>.=ZH=<color=@#Fighter#>戰士</color>戰士是戰士。他們幾乎可以使用所有武器。在第一級經驗之後，每公升 4 級經驗，戰士就會獲得<l="MAN_MELEE_COMBAT","extra attack"/>的戰鬥能力。 \n \n \n \n 有能力成為<l="MAN_GEOMANCER"/>。
Paladin=ZH=聖騎士
<color=@#Fighter#>Fighter</color>\n\nPaladins are fighters who have sworn to fight all evil and uphold honor and purity in all places and situations. Paladins can use most weapons including many that other fighters can't. Paladins get <l="MAN_MELEE_COMBAT","multiple attacks"/> at higher levels and also have greater resistance to evil magic.\n\n\n\nCapable of becoming a <l="MAN_GEOMANCER"/>.=ZH=<color=@#Fighter#>戰士</color>聖騎士是發誓在任何地方和情況下與一切邪惡作鬥爭並維護榮譽和純潔的戰士。聖騎士可以使用大多數武器，包括許多其他戰士無法使用的武器。聖騎士在更高等級時獲得<l="MAN_MELEE_COMBAT","multiple attacks"/>，並且對邪惡魔法也有更強的抵抗力。 \n \n \n \n 有能力成為<l="MAN_GEOMANCER"/>。
Rogue=ZH=盜賊
<color=@#Fighter#>Fighter</color>\n\nRogues are professional thieves with mediocre combat skills. However, rogues are experts at skulking in the shadows, as well as searching for <l="MAN_CHEST_TRAPS","chest traps"/> and disarming them. Without a rogue in the ranks of the party, booty will come at an unusually high price.\n\n\n\nWhen rogues successfully slip into the shadows to avoid combat, they advance 10 feet. If they are already cloaked in the shadows, they can continue to advance further and further. An attack from the shadows have a good chance of getting a <l="MAN_MELEE_COMBAT","critical hit"/> because they are striking the monster from behind.=ZH=<color=@#Fighter#>戰士</color>盜賊是戰鬥技能平庸的職業盜賊。然而，盜賊是潛伏在陰影中、尋找<l="MAN_CHEST_TRAPS","chest traps"/>並解除其武裝的專家。如果隊伍中沒有流氓，戰利品的價格就會異常高。 \n \n \n \n 當盜賊成功潛入陰影以避免戰鬥時，他們會前進 10 英尺。如果他們已經隱藏在陰影之中，他們還可以繼續前進，越走越遠。來自陰影的攻擊很有可能獲得<l="MAN_MELEE_COMBAT","critical hit"/>，因為它們是從後面攻擊怪物。
Bard=ZH=吟遊詩人
<color=@#Fighter#>Fighter</color><sprite=4/>\n\n<width=65%>The bard is a wandering minstrel. He is always seen with a tankard of ale in front of him in even the less reputable of taverns. The rowdier the better.\n\n\n\nMost bards are warriors who have given up the art of war for the art of <l="MAN_BARD_SONGS","magical music"/>. This doesn't mean that they have forgotten how to handle warrior weapons.</width>\n\nBut because they're not true warriors, bards don't get the advantage of extra attacks during combat. Their magic is so unique, it is almost impossible to survive without one.\n\n\n\nAny true bard has a variety of tunes on their lips, though to play them, they must have an instrument equipped and a healthy voice. When the going gets tough, the bard goes drinking.\n\n\n\nCapable of becoming a <l="MAN_GEOMANCER"/>.=ZH=<color=@#Fighter#>戰士</color><sprite=4/><width=65%>吟遊詩人是一位流浪的吟遊詩人。即使在信譽較差的小酒館裡，人們總是看到他面前放著一大杯麥芽酒。越吵越好。 \n \n \n \n 大多數吟遊詩人都是放棄兵法而轉向<l="MAN_BARD_SONGS","magical music"/>藝術的戰士。這並不意味著他們忘記瞭如何使用戰士武器。</width>但因為他們不是真正的戰士，吟遊詩人在戰鬥中無法獲得額外攻擊的優勢。他們的魔法是如此獨特，沒有魔法幾乎不可能生存。 \n \n \n \n 任何真正的吟遊詩人都會有各種各樣的曲調，但要演奏它們，他們必須擁有樂器和健康的聲音。當事情變得艱難時，吟遊詩人就會去喝酒。 \n \n \n \n 能夠成為<l="MAN_GEOMANCER"/>。
Hunter=ZH=獵人
<color=@#Fighter#>Fighter</color>\n\nThe Hunter is an assassin, a mercenary, but also a ninja. The hunter can use most weapons, and has the ability to do <l="MAN_MELEE_COMBAT","critical hits"/> in combat. As the hunter levels up so does the skill that increases critical hit chance.\n\n\n\nCapable of becoming a <l="MAN_GEOMANCER"/>.=ZH=<color=@#Fighter#>戰士</color>獵人是刺客、傭兵，同時也是忍者。獵人可以使用大部分武器，並且有能力在戰鬥中做到<l="MAN_MELEE_COMBAT","critical hits"/>。隨著獵人等級的提高，增加暴擊幾率的技能也會跟著提高。 \n \n \n \n 有能力成為<l="MAN_GEOMANCER"/>。
Monk=ZH=武僧
<color=@#Fighter#>Fighter</color>\n\nThe monk is a martial artist. An almost inhuman fighting machine trained to fight without weapons or armor. The monk can use them, but, at higher levels particularly, often does better without. Monks do well in combat because of their ability to attack <l="MAN_MELEE_COMBAT","multiple times"/>.\n\n\n\nCapable of becoming a <l="MAN_GEOMANCER"/>.=ZH=<color=@#Fighter#>戰士</color>武僧是武術家。近乎非人的戰鬥機器，經過訓練可以在沒有武器或裝甲的情況下進行戰鬥。武僧可以使用它們，但是，特別是在更高的級別，通常不使用它們會更好。武僧在戰鬥中表現出色，因為他們能夠攻擊<l="MAN_MELEE_COMBAT","multiple times"/>。 \n \n \n \n 有能力成為<l="MAN_GEOMANCER"/>。
Mages=ZH=法師
Mages are the types of classes that are best suited for magic based combat by casting <l="MAN_SPELLS","spells"/>.\n\n\n\nCasting spells uses <l="MAN_SPELL_POINTS","spell points"/> and spells require different amounts spell points. Magic users qualify to learn spells in the next <l="MAN_SPELL_LEVEL","spell level"/> when they are at an eligible <l="MAN_LEVEL","level"/>. It takes <l="MAN_GOLD","gold"/> to actually learn the new spells. Once a Magic User leaves a mage class, they can never return to it. Make sure to master each class before changing, to get full effectiveness out of a mage.\n\n\n\n<b>Tales of the Unknown (BT1)</b> starts with the classes - <l="MAN_CONJURER"/>, <l="MAN_MAGICIAN"/>, <l="MAN_SORCERER"/>, <l="MAN_WIZARD"/>.\n\n\n\n<b>The Destiny Knight (BT2)</b> introduces the - <l="MAN_ARCHMAGE"/> class.\n\n\n\n<b>Thief of Fate (BT3)</b> adds the - <l="MAN_CHRONOMANCER"/> and <l="MAN_GEOMANCER"/> classes.=ZH=法師是最適合透過施放<l="MAN_SPELLS","spells"/>進行基於魔法的戰鬥的職業類型。 \n \n \n \n 施展法術使用<l="MAN_SPELL_POINTS","spell points"/>且法術需要不同數量的法術點數。當魔法使用者達到合格的<l="MAN_LEVEL","level"/>時，他們就有資格在下一個<l="MAN_SPELL_LEVEL","spell level"/>學習法術。需要<l="MAN_GOLD","gold"/>才能真正學習新咒語。魔法師一旦離開法師職業，就永遠無法再回到該職業。確保在改變之前掌握每個職業，以充分發揮法師的作用。<b>未知故事 (BT1)</b>從類別開始 -<l="MAN_CONJURER"/>、<l="MAN_MAGICIAN"/>、<l="MAN_SORCERER"/>、<l="MAN_WIZARD"/>。<b>命運騎士 (BT2)</b>引入了 -<l="MAN_ARCHMAGE"/>類別。<b>命運之盜 (BT3)</b>新增了 -<l="MAN_CHRONOMANCER"/>和<l="MAN_GEOMANCER"/>類別。
Conjurer=ZH=魔術師
<color=@#MagicUser#>Magic User</color> (<l="MAN_CONJURER_SPELLS","@#MAN_SPELLS#"/>)\n\nConjuring deals with healing the wounded, instantaneous creation of physical phenomena and effects, through the channeling of a mage's power. Conjurer spells are potent, but not unlimited in effect, since the exercise of creation takes a large amount of energy to produce even a moderate effect. A conjurer can also produce effects that distort non-solid things to produce new effects, like the distortion of space and time required to teleport human bodies to a new location.\n\n\n\n<sprite=0/>=ZH=<color=@#MagicUser#>魔法使用者</color>(<l="MAN_CONJURER_SPELLS","@#MAN_SPELLS#"/>) \n \n 召喚術透過引導法師的力量來治療受傷、瞬間創造物理現象和效果。咒術師的法術很有效，但效果並不是無限的，因為創造的練習需要大量的能量才能產生即使是中等的效果。魔術師還可以產生扭曲非固體物體以產生新效果的效果，例如將人體傳送到新位置所需的空間和時間扭曲。<sprite=0/>
Magician=ZH=魔術師
<color=@#MagicUser#>Magic User</color> (<l="MAN_MAGICIAN_SPELLS","@#MAN_SPELLS#"/>)\n\nMagic, the art practiced by a Magician, deals with bestowing magical effects on common objects. The items do not become magical, but radiate fields of power for the duration of a spell. The gist of Magic is that it can increase an item's capability, give it a totally new capability, or change its form to something totally different.\n\n\n\n<pos=2.5f%><sprite=1/></pos>=ZH=<color=@#MagicUser#>魔法使用者</color>(<l="MAN_MAGICIAN_SPELLS","@#MAN_SPELLS#"/>) \n \n 魔法是魔術師所實踐的藝術，涉及對普通物體賦予魔法效果。這些物品不會變得具有魔法，但會在法術持續時間內散發出能量場。魔法的重點是它可以增加物品的能力，賦予它全新的能力，或將其形式改變為完全不同的東西。<pos=2.5f%><sprite=1/></pos>
Sorcerer=ZH=魔法師
<color=@#MagicUser#>Magic User</color> (<l="MAN_SORCERER_SPELLS","@#MAN_SPELLS#"/>)\n\nSorcery does not deal with a physical, manifestation, but rather the creation and manipulation of illusion and tapping into a heightened sense of awareness.\n\n<pos=70%><sprite=2/></pos>\n\n<width=67.5f%>An illusion is an image envisioned by the spell caster, which they then lay over the retinas of all who watch, augmenting this with appropriate stimulation of nerve endings to fool the other four senses. The human mind, believing an illusionary creation to be real, can be hurt or even killed by it, unless it is somehow disbelieved.<width=65%> A sorcerer, with the aid of their art, can also perceive certain special things hidden from view.</width>\n\n\n\nDue to the power of Sorcerer spells, this class is not available to newly created characters. To become a Sorcerer, the mage must first have achieved spell level 3 in one of the other magical arts.=ZH=<color=@#MagicUser#>魔法使用者</color>(<l="MAN_SORCERER_SPELLS","@#MAN_SPELLS#"/>) \n \n 巫術不涉及物理、表現，而是創造和操縱幻象並提高意識。<pos=70%><sprite=2/></pos><width=67.5f%>幻像是施法者想像的圖像，然後他們將其放置在所有觀看者的視網膜上，透過適當刺激神經末梢來增強這種圖像，以愚弄其他四種感官。人類的思想相信幻覺的創造物是真實的，可能會受到傷害，甚至被殺死，除非有人不相信它。<width=65%>巫師借助他們的藝術，也可以感知某些隱藏在視線之外的特殊事物。</width>由於巫師法術的威力，該職業不適用於新創建的角色。要成為魔法師，法師必須先在其他魔法藝術之一中達到法術等級 3。
Wizard=ZH=術士
<color=@#MagicUser#>Magic User</color> (<l="MAN_WIZARD_SPELLS","@#MAN_SPELLS#"/>)\n\nWizardry deals with the summoning and binding of various supernatural creatures. These creatures are not friendly to humankind and trying to control them is extremely hazardous.\n\n<pos=1%><sprite=3/></pos>\n\n<margin-left=25fem>The wizard has fewer spells than the other three classes, but theirs are by far the most potent. The creatures they can summon come from another plane of existence, called the negative plane. The summoned monsters will join party, and will fight until defeated. The wizard has some ability to trap and control normal monsters as well.</margin>\n\n\n\nDue to the power of Wizard spells, this class is not available to newly created characters. To become a Wizard, the mage must first have achieved spell level 3 in two other magical arts.=ZH=<color=@#MagicUser#>魔法使用者</color>(<l="MAN_WIZARD_SPELLS","@#MAN_SPELLS#"/>) \n \n 魔法涉及各種超自然生物的召喚和束縛。這些生物對人類並不友好，試圖控制它們是極其危險的。<pos=1%><sprite=3/></pos><margin-left=25fem>巫師的法術比其他三個職業少，但他們的法術是迄今為止最有效的。他們可以召喚的生物來自另一個存在位面，稱為負位面。召喚出來的怪物會加入隊伍，並戰鬥直到被擊敗。巫師也有一定的能力來捕捉和控制普通怪物。</margin>由於巫師法術的威力，此類不適用於新創建的角色。要成為巫師，法師必須先在另外兩種魔法藝術中達到法術等級 3。
Archmage=ZH=大法師
<color=@#MagicUser#>Magic User</color> (<l="MAN_ARCHMAGE_SPELLS","@#MAN_SPELLS#"/>)\n\nArchmage's are masters of magic. They are the most powerful form of mage because an archmage can do almost anything. These masters of magic have risen through at least three <l="MAN_SPELL_LEVEL","spell levels"/> for each of the previous four magic using classes - <b><l="MAN_CONJURER"/></b>, <b><l="MAN_MAGICIAN"/></b>, <b><l="MAN_SORCERER"/></b> and a <b><l="MAN_WIZARD"/></b>.\n\n\n\nArchmages possess the knowledge to cast all of the spells learnt from their previous classes.=ZH=<color=@#MagicUser#>魔法使用者</color>(<l="MAN_ARCHMAGE_SPELLS","@#MAN_SPELLS#"/>) \n \n 大法師是魔法大師。他們是法師中最強大的形式，因為大法師幾乎可以做任何事。這些魔法大師對於之前的四個魔法使用等級中的每一個都至少通過了三個<l="MAN_SPELL_LEVEL","spell levels"/>的提升 -<b><l="MAN_CONJURER"/></b>，<b><l="MAN_MAGICIAN"/></b>，<b><l="MAN_SORCERER"/></b>和<b><l="MAN_WIZARD"/></b>。 \n \n \n \n 大法師擁有施展從先前職業中學到的所有法術的知識。
Chronomancer=ZH=時空術士
<color=@#MagicUser#>Magic User</color> (<l="MAN_CHRONOMANCER_SPELLS","@#MAN_SPELLS#"/>)\n\nChronomancers are the key to distant lands. Chronomancers are the travel experts of magic users. They help the party move from dimension to dimension with their special teleport spells. They also have a number of vicious offensive spells. Before becoming a chronomancer, a magic user must be an archmage and have a spell level of 3 or higher.\n\n\n\nWhen a mage becomes a chronomancer, they lose the power to use all the spells they had previously learnt. It may sound unfair, but the chronomancer needs to focus all of their attention on the important dimension teleport spells.\n\n\n\nIn <b>The Thief of Fate</b> a Chronomancer is <uppercase>necessary</uppercase> to get around.=ZH=<color=@#MagicUser#>魔法使用者</color>(<l="MAN_CHRONOMANCER_SPELLS","@#MAN_SPELLS#"/>) \n \n 時空術士是通往遙遠國度的鑰匙。時空術士是魔法使用者的旅遊專家。他們用特殊的傳送咒語幫助隊伍從一個維度移動到另一個維度。他們也擁有許多惡毒的攻擊咒語。在成為時空法師之前，魔法使用者必須是大法師且法術等級為 3 或更高。 \n \n \n \n 當法師成為時空法師時，他們將失去使用之前學到的所有法術的能力。這聽起來可能不公平，但時空法師需要將所有註意力集中在重要的維度傳送法術上。 \n \n \n \n 在<b>命運之盜</b>中，時空術士是<uppercase>必需的</uppercase>才能四處走動。
Geomancer=ZH=大地術士
<color=@#MagicUser#>Magic User</color> (<l="MAN_GEOMANCER_SPELLS","@#MAN_SPELLS#"/>)\n\nThe most physically adept of all the spell casters, Geomancers can handle most weapons that fighters can. Their combative nature shows in their repertoire of predominantly offensive spells. Geomancers are fighters who want to convert to a life of magic. Once they choose to change, they can still use the armor, weapons, and magic items that the fighter classes use. Unfortunately, when a fighter becomes a geomancer, they lose some class specific traits.\n\n\n\n<l="MAN_WARRIOR","Warriors"/> lose their <l="MAN_MELEE_COMBAT","multiple attacks"/>.\n\n<l="MAN_PALADIN","Paladins"/> lose their <l="MAN_MELEE_COMBAT","multiple attacks"/>.\n\n<l="MAN_MONK","Monks"/> lose their <l="MAN_MELEE_COMBAT","multiple attacks"/> and <l="MAN_ARMOR_CLASS","armor class"/> bonus.\n\n<l="MAN_BARD","Bards"/> lose their <l="MAN_BARD_SONGS","songs"/>.\n\n<l="MAN_HUNTER","Hunters"/> lose their <l="MAN_MELEE_COMBAT","critical hit"/> ability.\n\n\n\nIt is a part of the trade off. To become a geomancer, a special location must be found. It is the only place the rite of passage to become a Geomancer can be performed.=ZH=<color=@#MagicUser#>魔法使用者</color>(<l="MAN_GEOMANCER_SPELLS","@#MAN_SPELLS#"/>) \n \n 大地術士是所有施法者中身體最熟練的，可以使用戰士可以使用的大多數武器。他們的好鬥本質體現在他們主要使用進攻性法術的技能上。大地術士是想要轉變為魔法生活的戰士。一旦他們選擇改變，他們仍然可以使用戰士職業使用的盔甲、武器和魔法物品。不幸的是，當戰士成為大地術士時，他們會失去一些職業特定的特徵。<l="MAN_WARRIOR","Warriors"/>失去了他們的<l="MAN_MELEE_COMBAT","multiple attacks"/>。<l="MAN_PALADIN","Paladins"/>失去了他們的<l="MAN_MELEE_COMBAT","multiple attacks"/>。<l="MAN_MONK","Monks"/>失去<l="MAN_MELEE_COMBAT","multiple attacks"/>和<l="MAN_ARMOR_CLASS","armor class"/>獎金。<l="MAN_BARD","Bards"/>失去了他們的<l="MAN_BARD_SONGS","songs"/>。<l="MAN_HUNTER","Hunters"/>失去他們的<l="MAN_MELEE_COMBAT","critical hit"/>能力。 \n \n \n \n 這是權衡的一部分。要成為大地術士，必須找到一個特別的地點。這是唯一可以舉行大地術士成年儀式的地方。
Attributes=ZH=屬性
Each character has five basic attributes which define their physical and mental prowess.\n\n<b><l="MAN_STRENGTH"/></b>\n\n<b><l="MAN_INTELLIGENCE"/></b>\n\n<b><l="MAN_DEXTERITY"/></b>\n\n<b><l="MAN_CONSTITUTION"/></b>\n\n<b><l="MAN_LUCK"/></b>\n\n\n\nEvery character also has other <b><l="MAN_STATISTICS","statistics"/></b>.\n\n\n\nWhen a character is created, each attribute is randomly assigned a value from a <l="MAN_RACE","races'"/> base <l="MAN_BASE_RACIAL_ATTRIBUTES","racial attribute"/> value, to a maximum of 18. Higher numbers reflect higher capability. These stats can be re-rolled before confirming and applying these to the character.=ZH=每個角色都有五個基本屬性，定義了他們的身體和精神力量。<b><l="MAN_STRENGTH"/></b><b><l="MAN_INTELLIGENCE"/></b><b><l="MAN_DEXTERITY"/></b><b><l="MAN_CONSTITUTION"/></b><b><l="MAN_LUCK"/></b>每個角色還有其他<b>16Y</b>6Y。 \n \n \n \n 建立角色時，每個屬性都會從<l="MAN_RACE","races'"/>基礎<l="MAN_BASE_RACIAL_ATTRIBUTES","racial attribute"/>值隨機分配一個值，最大為 18。數字越大，能力越高。在確認這些統計數據並將其應用到角色之前，可以重新捲動這些統計數據。

Each <l="MAN_RACE","race"/> has base value <l="MAN_ATTRIBUTES","attributes"/>. These values act as a minimum value for an attribute roll and will never be less than the base value. Consider making characters races which have high base attributes that best benefit their <l="MAN_CLASS","class"/>.\n\n\n\n<size=125%><color=@#Heading#><align="center"><b>Base Strength=ZH=力量
Strength is physical power and chiefly affects the amount of damage a character can do to an opponent in <l="MAN_MELEE_COMBAT","melee combat"/>. Make sure <color=@#Fighter#>fighters</color> are strong.=ZH=力量是物理力量，主要影響角色在<l="MAN_MELEE_COMBAT","melee combat"/>中可以對對手造成的傷害量。確保<color=@#Fighter#>戰士</color>強大。
Intelligence=ZH=智力
Intelligence is mental power. A high intelligence rating will enable <color=@#MagicUser#>magic users</color> to get bonus <l="MAN_SPELL_POINTS","spell points"/>.=ZH=智力是精神力量。高智力等級將使<color=@#MagicUser#>魔法使用者</color>獲得獎勵<l="MAN_SPELL_POINTS","spell points"/>。
Dexterity=ZH=敏捷
Dexterity measures agility and nimbleness. A high Dexterity makes characters harder to hit and helps them strike the first blow in <l="MAN_COMBAT","combat"/>.=ZH=敏捷性衡量敏捷性和敏捷性。高敏捷度使角色更難被擊中，並幫助他們在<l="MAN_COMBAT","combat"/>中發動第一擊。
Constitution=ZH=體質
Constitution measures healthiness and survivability. It takes more damage to kill a character with a high constitution. Constitution is reflected with bonus <b><l="MAN_HIT_POINTS","hit points"/></b>.=ZH=體質衡量健康和生存能力。殺死體質高的角色需要更多的傷害。體質透過獎金<b><l="MAN_HIT_POINTS","hit points"/></b>體現。
Luck=ZH=運氣
Luck is ambiguous, unpredictable, and has a number of unforeseen effects on characters' lives. Lucky characters are more likely to resist evil magic and avoid <l="MAN_TRAPS","traps"/> and <l="MAN_CHEST_TRAPS","chest traps"/>.=ZH=運氣是模糊的、不可預測的，並且會對角色的生活產生許多不可預見的影響。幸運的角色更有可能抵抗邪惡魔法並避免<l="MAN_TRAPS","traps"/>和<l="MAN_CHEST_TRAPS","chest traps"/>。
Statistics=ZH=統計數據
Each character also has statistics dependant on <b><l="MAN_CLASS","class"/></b>.\n\n<b><l="MAN_LEVEL"/></b>\n\n<b><l="MAN_HIT_POINTS"/></b>\n\n<b><l="MAN_SPELL_POINTS"/></b>\n\n<b><l="MAN_EXPERIENCE"/></b>\n\n<b><l="MAN_ARMOR_CLASS"/></b>=ZH=每個字元還具有依賴<b><l="MAN_CLASS","class"/></b>的統計資料。<b><l="MAN_LEVEL"/></b><b><l="MAN_HIT_POINTS"/></b><b><l="MAN_SPELL_POINTS"/></b><b><l="MAN_EXPERIENCE"/></b><b><l="MAN_ARMOR_CLASS"/></b>
Level=ZH=等級
Level is a general measure of achievement within a character's class.\n\n\n\nThe <b><l="MAN_REVIEW_BOARD","Review board"/></b> will promote a character to higher levels based on their <b><l="MAN_EXPERIENCE","experience"/></b>, but only upon the character's in-person request. Advancement in levels is very important because it generally means an increase in <b><l="MAN_ATTRIBUTES","attributes"/></b>.=ZH=等級是角色職業中成就的一般衡量標準。<b><l="MAN_REVIEW_BOARD","Review board"/></b>將根據角色的<b><l="MAN_EXPERIENCE","experience"/></b>將角色提升到更高級別，但僅根據角色的親自請求。等級的提升非常重要，因為它通常意味著<b><l="MAN_ATTRIBUTES","attributes"/></b>的增加。
Experience (XP)=ZH=經驗（XP）
Experience (XP) measures abilities gained by experience, the higher the number, the better the character. Characters are <l="MAN_CHESTS","awarded"/> XP for successful combat.\n\n\n\nXP is used to <b><l="MAN_LEVEL","level"/></b> characters up. When a character has enough XP to reach the next level, an arrow will appear on the left of a character's name in the <l="MAN_PARTY_STATUS","party status"/>.\n\n\n\n<pos=40f%>@#AC#</pos><pos=55.5f%><color=#141414>@#HP#</color></pos><pos=78f%>@#SP#</pos><pos=95.5f%>@#CL#</pos>\n\n<pos=0.25f%>1</pos><pos=2%><sprite=63/></pos><pos=5.5f%>@#Brian the Armor Class (AC)=ZH=裝甲等級（AC）
Armor Class reflects the level of protection a character has from physical attacks. Armor class starts at 10, the lower the armor class the better.\n\n\n\nAs the character's protection improves through armor, spells, and other means, the armor class drops to a maximum of -50.=ZH=防禦等級反映了角色免受物理攻擊的保護程度。護甲等級從10開始，護甲等級越低越好。 \n \n \n \n 隨著角色的防護能力透過護甲、法術和其他方式提高，護甲等級會下降到最大值 -50。
Hit Points (HP)=ZH=生命值（HP）
Hit Points (@#HP#) measure the damage a character can take before they are killed.\n\n\n\nThe @#HP# is represented by <color=#141414>(current health)/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>(maximum health)</color>\n\n<color=#141414>126/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>258</color>\n\n\n\nIf a party members current @#HP# is 0 or less. They are <l="MAN_CHARACTER_CONDITIONS","dead"/>.\n\n<color=#141414>0/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF><uppercase>dead</uppercase></color>\n\n\n\n@#HP# also shows any <l="MAN_CHARACTER_CONDITIONS","condition"/> that the character has.\n\n\n\nCharacter's details in the <l="MAN_PARTY_STATUS","party status"/> will cause the party slot line of the party member, to flash a color when something is affecting them.\n\n\n\nBeing hit or taking damage - flashes red.\n\n<pos=0.25f%>1</pos><pos=2.5f%><mark=#A100003C>@#Brian the Fist#</pos><pos=25f%>-10</pos><pos=35f%><color=#141414>108/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>258</color></pos><pos=50f%></pos><pos=65f%>@#WA#</pos></mark>\n\n<pos=0f%>2</pos><pos=2.5f%><mark=#A100003C>@#El Cid#</pos><pos=25f%>-8</pos><pos=35f%><color=#141414>215/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>226</color></pos><pos=50f%></pos><pos=65f%>@#PA#</pos></mark>\n\n\n\nRegenerating @#HP# or being healed - flashes yellow.\n\n<pos=0.25f%>1</pos><pos=2.5f%><mark=#FFC91928>@#Brian the Fist#</pos><pos=25f%>-10</pos><pos=35f%><color=#141414>108/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>258</color></pos><pos=50f%></pos><pos=65f%>@#WA#</pos></mark>\n\n<pos=0f%>2</pos><pos=2.5f%><mark=#FFC91928>@#El Cid#</pos><pos=25f%>-8</pos><pos=35f%><color=#141414>215/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>226</color></pos><pos=50f%></pos><pos=65f%>@#PA#</pos></mark>\n\n<pos=0f%>5</pos><pos=2.5f%><mark=#FFC91928>@#Merlin#</pos><pos=25f%>-1</pos><pos=35f%><color=#141414>145/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>145</color><pos=50f%></pos><color=#141414>409/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>512</color><pos=50f%></pos><pos=65f%>@#CO#</pos></mark>\n\n<pos=0f%>6</pos><pos=2.5f%><mark=#FFC91928>@#Omar#</pos><pos=25.5f%>2</pos><pos=35f%><color=#141414>163/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>163</color></pos><pos=50f%></pos><color=#141414>367/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>571</color><pos=65f%>@#MA#</pos></mark>\n\n\n\nTaking damage from being <l="MAN_CHARACTER_CONDITIONS","poisoned"/> - Spell Points (SP)=ZH=法術點數 (SP)
Spell Points (@#SP#) are used to cast <l="MAN_SPELLS","magic spells"/>. @#SP# are used up with each cast of a magic spell. Spells have a required amount of @#SP# to be cast. If a magic users @#SP# do no meet the requirements of the spell, the <color=#991A1AFF>spell name will be red</color>, it will fizzle - and not be cast.\n\n\n\nSpell points are regenerated automatically when a magic user is in a city or out in a wilderness in the daytime. Spell points may also be regenerated in <l="MAN_ROSCOES_ENERGY_EMPORIUM"/> or special <l="MAN_DUNGEON_REGENERATION","spell point regeneration"/> tiles in some <l="MAN_DUNGEONS","dungeons"/>.\n\n\n\nThe @#SP# is represented by <color=#141414>(current spell points)/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>(maximum spell points)</color>\n\n<color=#141414>64/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>128</color>\n\n\n\nA character’s party slot will flash blue when spell points are regenerated.\n\n<pos=0f%>5</pos><pos=2.5f%><mark=#0069D22D>@#Merlin#</pos><pos=25f%>-1</pos><pos=35f%><color=#141414>145/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>145</color><pos=50f%></pos><color=#141414>409/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>512</color><pos=50f%></pos><pos=65f%>@#CO#</pos></mark>\n\n<pos=0f%>6</pos><pos=2.5f%><mark=#0069D22D>@#Omar#</pos><pos=25.5f%>2</pos><pos=35f%><color=#141414>163/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>163</color></pos><pos=50f%></pos><color=#141414>367/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>571</color><pos=65f%>@#MA#</pos></mark>=ZH=法術點（@#SP#）用於施放<l="MAN_SPELLS","magic spells"/>。 @#SP# 會在每次施放魔法咒語時耗盡。法術需要施放一定數量的@#SP#。如果魔法使用者@#SP#不符合咒語的要求，則<color=#991A1AFF>咒語名稱將為紅色</color>，它將失敗 - 並且不會被施放。 \n \n \n \n 當魔法師白天在城市或野外時，法術點會自動重新生成。法術點也可以在<l="MAN_ROSCOES_ENERGY_EMPORIUM"/>或某些<l="MAN_DUNGEONS","dungeons"/>中的特殊<l="MAN_DUNGEON_REGENERATION","spell point regeneration"/>方塊中重新產生。 \n \n \n \n @#SP# 由<color=#141414>（當前法術點）/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>（最大法術點）</color><color=#141414>64/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>128</color>重新生成法術點時，角色的隊伍閃爍角色會閃爍藍色。<pos=0f%>5</pos><pos=2.5f%><mark=#0069D22D>@#梅林#</pos><pos=25f%>-1</pos><pos=35f%><color=#141414>145/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>145</color><pos=50f%></pos><color=#141414>409/</color>3<l="MAN_ROSCOES_ENERGY_EMPORIUM"/>3Y3 @#CO#</pos></mark><pos=0f%>6</pos><pos=2.5f%><mark=#0069D22D>@#奧馬爾#</pos><pos=25.5f%>2</pos><pos=35f%><color=#141414>163/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>163</color></pos><pos=50f%></color><pos=65f%>@#MA#</pos></mark>
Spell Level=ZH=法術等級
Spell level is the highest tier of magic spells a character can use in any given school of magic. There are 7 spell levels of magic for each <l="MAN_MAGES","mage class"/>. To be eligible to learn a new level of magic spells, a magic user must advance two <l="MAN_LEVEL","levels"/>.<line-height=120%>\n\n</line-height><margin=15fem>Level<pos=42%>Spell Level</pos></margin>\n\n<margin=17em>1<pos=45.5%></pos>1\n\n2<pos=45.5%></pos>1\n\n3<pos=45.5%></pos>2<sprite=64/>\n\n4<pos=45.5%></pos>2\n\n5<pos=45.5%></pos>3<sprite=64/>\n\n6<pos=45.5%></pos>3\n\n7<pos=45.5%></pos>4<sprite=64/>\n\n8<pos=45.5%></pos>4\n\n9<pos=45.5%></pos>5<sprite=64/>\n\n10<pos=45.5%></pos>5 \n\n11<pos=45.5%></pos>6<sprite=64/>\n\n12<pos=45.5%></pos>6\n\n13+<pos=45.5%></pos>7<sprite=64/></margin><line-height=120%>\n\n</line-height>When a character is at a level where they are eligible to advance their spell level, a star will appear on the left of a character's name in the <l="MAN_PARTY_STATUS","party status"/>.\n\n\n\n<pos=0f%>5</pos><pos=2%><sprite=64/></pos><pos=5.5f%>@#Merlin#</pos><pos=40f%>-1</pos><pos=53f%><color=#141414>145/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>145</color></pos><pos=75f%></pos><color=#141414>409/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>512</color><pos=95f%>@#CO#</pos>\n\n<pos=0f%>6</pos><pos=2%><sprite=64/></pos><pos=5.5f%>@#Omar#</pos><pos=40.5f%>2</pos><pos=53f%><color=#141414>163/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>163</color></pos><pos=75f%></pos><color=#141414>367/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>571</color><pos=95f%>@#MA#</pos>\n\n\n\nMages who have learnt at least 3 spell levels (<l="MAN_LEVEL","level"/> 5+) in an art may elect to change to another mage class at any time. A mage changing class will have very basic knowledge of the class they are new to learning. However they retain all current <l="MAN_ATTRIBUTES","attributes"/>, <l="MAN_STATISTICS","statistics"/> and all knowledge of spells they have practiced in all previous classes. \n\n\n\nOnce a magic user leaves a mage class, they can never return to it. Make sure to master each class before changing, to get full effectiveness out of a mage.=ZH=法術等級是角色在任何給定魔法學校中可以使用的最高等級的魔法法術。每個<l="MAN_MAGES","mage class"/>有 7 個魔法法術等級。為了有資格學習新等級的魔法咒語，魔法使用者必須前進兩個<l="MAN_LEVEL","levels"/>。<line-height=120%></line-height><margin=15fem>等級<pos=42%>法術等級</pos></margin><margin=17em>1<pos=45.5%></pos>1 \n \n 2<pos=45.5%></pos>1 \n \n 3<pos=45.5%></pos>2<sprite=64/>4<pos=45.5%>YNN<sprite=64/>6<pos=45.5%></pos>3 \n \n 7<pos=45.5%></pos>4<sprite=64/>8<pos=45.5%></pos>4 \n \n 9<pos=45.5%></pos>5<sprite=64/>10<pos=45.5%></pos></pos>5<sprite=64/>10<pos=45.5%></pos>5 \n 12<pos=45.5%></pos>6 \n \n 13+<pos=45.5%></pos>7<sprite=64/></margin><line-height=120%></line-height>當角色達到有資格提升法術等級的等級時，<l="MAN_PARTY_STATUS","party status"/>中角色名字的左側會出現一顆星。<pos=0f%>5</pos><pos=2%><sprite=64/></pos><pos=5.5f%>@#Merlin#</pos><pos=40f%>-1</pos><pos=53f%><color=#141414>145/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>145</color></pos><pos=75f%></color><pos=95f%>@#CO#</pos><pos=0f%>6</pos><pos=2%><sprite=64/></pos><pos=5.5f%>@#Omar#</pos><pos=40.5f%>2</pos><pos=53f%><color=#141414>163/</color><pos=40.5f%>2</pos><pos=53f%><color=#141414>163/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>163Y<pos=53f%><color=#141414>163/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>163 Y81 Y83 YY3838 YY</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>571</color><pos=95f%>@#MA#</pos>在某門藝術中學習了至少 3 個法術等級（<l="MAN_LEVEL","level"/>5+）的法師可以隨時選擇更換為另一個法師職業。法師轉換職業將對他們新學習的職業有非常基本的知識。然而，他們保留了所有當前的<l="MAN_ATTRIBUTES","attributes"/>、<l="MAN_STATISTICS","statistics"/>以及他們在之前所有職業中練習過的所有法術知識。  \n \n \n \n 魔法使用者一旦離開法師職業，就永遠無法返回。確保在改變之前掌握每個職業，以充分發揮法師的作用。
Conditions=ZH=狀況
Conditions affect characters and monsters. Conditions hinder abilities in and out of battle. Conditions are inflicted by special attacks, spells, or <l="MAN_TRAPS","traps"/>. Each condition has its own effect and usually lingers until they are cured at a <l="MAN_TEMPLE","temple"/> or by <l="MAN_SPELLS","magic spells"/>. These conditions cover the maximum health text of a character on party roster, and are abbreviated to a code no longer than 5 letters. (<color=#141414>current health</color>)/(<color=#F0F0F0FF><uppercase>cond</uppercase></color>)\n\n\n\n<pos=0.25f%>1</pos><pos=2.5f%>@#Brian the Fist#</pos><pos=25f%>-10</pos><pos=35f%><color=#141414>108/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>258</color></pos><pos=50f%></pos><pos=65f%>@#WA#</pos>\n\n<pos=0f%>5</pos><pos=2.5f%>@#Merlin#</pos><pos=25f%>-1</pos><pos=35f%><color=#141414>145/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>145</color><pos=50f%></pos><color=#141414>409/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>512</color><pos=50f%></pos><pos=65f%>@#CO#</pos>\n\n\n\n<smallcaps>Death</smallcaps> - <color=#F0F0F0FF>DEAD</color>\n\nDead - not alive. This party member has had their health drop to 0. A dead party member can not act in any way. Resurrected by - <u>Temple & Spells</u>.\n\n\n\n<smallcaps>Poison</smallcaps> - <color=#F0F0F0FF>POIS</color>\n\nHit points are slowly drained. This includes while moving and after each round of combat. Cured by - <u>Temple & Spells</u>.\n\n\n\n<smallcaps>Petrification</smallcaps> - <color=#F0F0F0FF>STON</color>\n\nBeing turned to stone effectively kills the character twice - they will drop to the bottom of the roster, and after being un-petrified they will still need to be resurrected. Cured by - <u>Temple & Spells</u>.\n\n\n\n<smallcaps>Insanity</smallcaps> - <color=#F0F0F0FF>NUTS</color>\n\nInsanity causes the loss of control of actions. Insane party members will perform random actions in and out of combat. It is common for the insane to attack other party members at any time. Cured by - <u>Temple & Spells</u>.\n\n\n\n<smallcaps>Paralysis</smallcaps> - <color=#F0F0F0FF>PARA</color>\n\nImmobilized from performing actions, including combat. Cured by - <u>Temple & Spells</u>.\n\n\n\n<smallcaps>Possession</smallcaps> - <color=#F0F0F0FF>POSS</color>\n\nBeing possessed turns the party member against the party. In combat possessed party members will attack members within the party. When not in combat, they will initiate a party attack. Cured by - <u>Temple & Spells</u>.\n\n\n\n<smallcaps>Drained</smallcaps> - <color=#F0F0F0FF>DRAIN</color>\n\nThe character's level is drained, reducing their level by 1 and reducing their maximum @#HP#. Cured by - <u>Temple</u>.\n\n\n\n<smallcaps>Withering</smallcaps> - <color=#F0F0F0FF>OLD</color>\n\nAll attributes are reduced to 1. Cured by - <u>Temple & Spells</u>.\n\n\n\n<smallcaps>Stunned</smallcaps> - <color=#F0F0F0FF>STUN</color>\n\nStunned party members miss a round in combat and perform no actions. Cured by - <u>Time</u>.\n\n\n\n<smallcaps>Hidden in Shadows</smallcaps> - <color=#F0F0F0FF>HIDE</color>\n\nParty members hidden in shadows are cloaked and practically invisible, they can not be targeted for attack. Cancelled by - <u>Performing an attack</u>.=ZH=條件會影響角色和怪物。條件會阻礙戰鬥內外的能力。特殊攻擊、法術或<l="MAN_TRAPS","traps"/>會造成狀況。每種情況都有其自身的影響，並且通常會持續到<l="MAN_TEMPLE","temple"/>或<l="MAN_SPELLS","magic spells"/>治癒為止。這些條件涵蓋了隊伍名冊上角色的最大健康狀況文本，並縮寫為不超過 5 個字母的代碼。 (<color=#141414>當前生命值</color>)/(<color=#F0F0F0FF><uppercase>狀況</uppercase></color>)<pos=0.25f%>1</pos><pos=2.5f%>@#布萊恩拳頭#</pos><pos=25f%>-10</pos><pos=35f%><color=#141414>108/</color>18 YY20<pos=65f%>@#WA#</pos><pos=0f%>5</pos><pos=2.5f%>@#梅林#</pos><pos=25f%>-1</pos><pos=35f%><color=#141414>145/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>145</color><pos=50f%></pos><color=#141414>400<color=#141414>40Y2</pos><pos=65f%>@#CO#</pos><smallcaps>死亡</smallcaps>-<color=#F0F0F0FF>死亡</color>死亡 - 沒有活著。該隊員的生命值已降至 0。死亡的隊員無法進行任何行動。由 -<u>聖殿與法術</u>復活。<smallcaps>毒藥</smallcaps>-<color=#F0F0F0FF>POIS</color>生命值緩慢耗盡。這包括移動時和每輪戰鬥之後。由 -<u>聖殿與法術</u>治癒。<smallcaps>石化</smallcaps>-<color=#F0F0F0FF>石頭</color>變成石頭會有效地殺死角色兩次 - 他們會掉到名單的底部，在取消石化後，他們仍然需要復活。由 -<u>聖殿與法術</u>治癒。<smallcaps>精神錯亂</smallcaps>-<color=#F0F0F0FF>堅果</color>精神錯亂會導致行動失控。瘋狂的隊員會在戰鬥和戰鬥外執行隨機動作。瘋子隨時攻擊其他隊員是很常見的事情。由 -<u>聖殿與法術</u>治癒。<smallcaps>癱瘓</smallcaps>-<color=#F0F0F0FF>PARA</color>無法執行動作，包括戰鬥。由 -<u>聖殿與法術</u>治癒。<smallcaps>佔有</smallcaps>-<color=#F0F0F0FF>POSS</color>被佔有會使隊員反對黨。在戰鬥中，被附身的隊員會攻擊黨內的成員。當不在戰鬥中時，他們會發動隊伍攻擊。由 -<u>聖殿與法術</u>治癒。<smallcaps>耗盡</smallcaps>-<color=#F0F0F0FF>耗盡</color>角色的等級被耗盡，將其等級降低 1 並降低最大@#HP#。由 -<u>Temple</u>治癒。<smallcaps>枯萎</smallcaps>-<color=#F0F0F0FF>老</color>所有屬性減少至 1。治癒者 -<u>神殿與法術</u>。<smallcaps>昏迷</smallcaps>-<color=#F0F0F0FF>STUN</color>昏迷的隊員在戰鬥中錯過一輪且沒有執行任何操作。固化時間：<u>時間</u>。<smallcaps>潛伏於陰影</smallcaps>-<color=#F0F0F0FF>隱藏</color>潛伏於陰影的隊員處於隱形狀態，幾乎看不見，他們無法成為攻擊目標。取消者 -<u>執行攻擊</u>。
Spells=ZH=法術
Spells are used for a multitude of reasons, including - to damage, heal, summon, bolster, navigate, defend, explore and disarm. Spells are the primary way that <l="MAN_MAGES","mages"/> inflict damage in <l="MAN_COMBAT","combat"/>. In order to advance in <l="MAN_SPELL_LEVEL","spell level"/>, the mage must be an appropriate <l="MAN_LEVEL","level"/>.\n\n\n\nSpells can be searched for by a spell code, through filters, or navigating and selecting with the mouse.\n\n\n\nSpells have the ability to fizzle. Fizzling is when a spell is cast, the spell points are deducted from the mage, but the effect of the spell does not work. There are multiple reasons why a spell could fizzle.\n\n\n\nA spell consists of - a code, cost (<l="MAN_SPELL_POINTS","spell points"/>), target group, range, duration and effect.\n\n\n\n<color=@#SPELL_HEADER_COL#><pos=0%/>Code<pos=@#COL_COST#/>Cost<pos=@#COL_RANGE#/>Target Group/Range<pos=@#COL_DURATION#/>Duration</pos></color><line-height=130%>\n\n<color=@#SPELL_HEADER_COL#><pos=0%/>@#CODE_ARFI#<pos=@#COL_COST#/>3 @#SP#<pos=@#COL_RANGE#/>1 @#FOE# (10')<pos=@#COL_DURATION#/>@#COMBAT#</pos></color>\n\n<b>@#SPELL_ArcFire#:</b> @#SPELL_ArcFire_DESC,1,4#=ZH=法術有多種用途，包括傷害、治療、召喚、支援、導航、防禦、探索和解除。法術是<l="MAN_MAGES","mages"/>對<l="MAN_COMBAT","combat"/>造成傷害的主要方式。為了在<l="MAN_SPELL_LEVEL","spell level"/>中前進，法師必須是合適的<l="MAN_LEVEL","level"/>。 \n \n \n \n 可以透過拼字程式碼、過濾器或使用滑鼠導航和選擇來搜尋拼字。 \n \n \n \n 法術有失敗的能力。 Fizzling是施放法術時，法師的法術點數被扣除，但法術效果不起作用。咒語失敗的原因有很多。 \n \n \n \n 法術由 - 代碼、成本 (<l="MAN_SPELL_POINTS","spell points"/>)、目標組、範圍、持續時間和效果組成。<color=@#SPELL_HEADER_COL#><pos=0%/>代碼<pos=@#COL_COST#/>成本<pos=@#COL_RANGE#/>目標組/範圍<pos=@#COL_DURATION#/>持續時間</pos></color><line-height=130%><color=@#SPELL_HEADER_COL#><pos=0%/>@#CODE_ARFI#<pos=@#COL_COST#/>3 @#SP#<pos=@#COL_RANGE#/>1 @#FOE#10000 \nYYNN<b>@#SPELL_ArcFire#:</b>@#SPELL_ArcFire_DESC,1,4#
Bard Songs=ZH=吟遊詩人歌曲
The Bard makes magic by playing music. They need an equipped musical instrument, of course. They can play in combat or during exploration, with different <l="MAN_BARD_SONG_EFFECTS","effect"/>. A Bard can play as many tunes as they have experience <l="MAN_LEVEL","levels"/>, before their throat gets dry. Then they need to get a drink from any nearby <l="MAN_TAVERN","tavern"/> to rejuvenate their voice. Tough duty, but someone has to do it.\n\n\n\nA song played as the party is exploring is long-lasting and continues into <b><l="MAN_COMBAT","combat"/></b> and remains after the party returns, even if other songs were played during. After combat has ended, any songs and effects gained by playing during the battle, vanish.\n\n\n\nSongs played during combat have an abbreviated effect, creating different magical effects than the full non-combat versions. Some Bard songs can be played multiple times in combat - stacking the effect. Bard song strength varies with the bards level.\n\n\n\nOnly one Bard tune can be played at a time outside of combat. If a second one is played while the first is still playing (by the same or a different Bard), the first will end. \n\n\n\n@#BARD_STARTING_SONGS#=ZH=吟遊詩人透過演奏音樂來施展魔法。當然，他們需要配備齊全的樂器。他們可以在戰鬥或探索過程中以不同的<l="MAN_BARD_SONG_EFFECTS","effect"/>進行遊戲。吟遊詩人可以在喉嚨變乾之前演奏盡可能多的曲子，因為他們有經驗<l="MAN_LEVEL","levels"/>。然後他們需要從附近的<l="MAN_TAVERN","tavern"/>那裡喝一杯來恢復他們的聲音。任務艱鉅，但必須有人去做。 \n \n \n \n 隊伍探索時播放的歌曲是永久，會持續到<b><l="MAN_COMBAT","combat"/></b>，並在隊伍返回後保留，即使期間播放了其他歌曲。戰鬥結束後，在戰鬥中播放的任何歌曲和效果都會消失。 \n \n \n \n 戰鬥中播放的歌曲具有縮寫效果，與完整的非戰鬥版本相比，會產生不同的魔法效果。一些吟遊詩人歌曲可以在戰鬥中多次播放 - 疊加效果。吟遊詩人歌曲的強度會隨著吟遊詩人的等級而改變。 \n \n \n \n 在戰鬥之外一次只能演奏一首吟遊詩人曲子。如果在第一個仍在播放時播放第二個（由相同或不同的吟遊詩人），則第一個將結束。  \n \n \n \n @#BARD_STARTING_SONGS#
Song Effects=ZH=歌曲效果
Inventory=ZH=庫存
The party has a shared inventory space of <u>40</u>. When the inventory is full, no more items will be placed in it, until spaces are available. Visit <l="MAN_GARTHS_SHOPPE"/> to sell some wares, free up inventory space and make a little gold in the meanwhile.\n\n\n\nAn item which has been equipped is marked in white and is above the party inventory text. Items below the party inventory line are not equipped. Some items can only be used by specific characters. <l="MAN_ITEMS","Unidentified items"/> and an item which can never be used by a character is marked in red.<pos=75%><sprite=30/></pos>\n\n<color="white">Item</color> - Equipped Item.\n\n<color="black">Item</color> - Unequipped Item.\n\n<color=@#CantEquip#>Item</color> - Unidentified or class can not use.\n\n\n\n<color="white">Weapon</color>\n\n<color="white">Armor</color>\n\n<color="white">Gloves</color>\n\n<color=#664C33>@#PARTY_INVENTORY#</color>\n\n<color="black">Lamp (1)</color>\n\n<color="black">Lamp (1)</color>\n\n<color=@#CantEquip#>Unidentified Item</color>\n\n<color=@#CantEquip#>Unidentified Item</color>\n\n<color=@#CantEquip#>Unidentified Weapon</color>=ZH=該方的共享庫存空間為<u>40</u>。當庫存已滿時，將不再放置任何物品，直到有可用空間。造訪<l="MAN_GARTHS_SHOPPE"/>出售一些商品，釋放庫存空間，同時賺取一點金幣。 \n \n \n \n 已裝備的物品以白色標記，位於隊伍物品欄文字上方。隊伍庫存線以下的物品未裝備。有些物品只能由特定角色使用。<l="MAN_ITEMS","Unidentified items"/>和角色永遠無法使用的物品被標記為紅色。<pos=75%><sprite=30/></pos><color="white">物品</color>- 裝備物品。<color="black">物品</color>- 未裝備的物品。<color=@#CantEquip#>項目</color>- 未知或類別無法使用。<color="white">武器</color><color="white">盔甲</color><color="white">手套</color><color=#664C33>@#PARTY_INVENTORY#</color><color="black">燈 (1)</color>YNN YNN YNND YNNY23 \n YNN YNNY251 \n YNN YNNY230</color><color=@#CantEquip#>不明物品</color><color=@#CantEquip#>不明武器</color>
Items=ZH=物品
There are 11 different item types. Characters have 11 item slots. Only one item of each type can be equipped for use at any one time. Item types fall under 3 categories -\n\n\n\n<smallcaps>Offense<pos=20%>Defense</pos><pos=40%>Item</pos></smallcaps>\n\nWeapons<pos=20%>Armor</pos><pos=40%>Instrument</pos>\n\nOffhand<pos=20%>Helm</pos><pos=40%>General</pos>\n\nArrow<pos=20%>Gloves</pos><pos=40%>Figurine</pos>\n\n<pos=20%>Ring</pos>\n\n<pos=20%>Boots</pos>\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>Thief of Fate Specific</b></color>\n\nThere are two special item types which have special item slots in the the third of the trilogy -\n\nSuit\n\nContainer\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>Unidentified Items & Specials</b></color>\n\n<color=@#CantEquip#>Unidentified items</color> can be identified by a <l="MAN_ROGUE"/> or <l="MAN_GARTHS_SHOPPE"/>.
